kuṟaiyavum vēṇṭip pali celuttit teyvatam paravuvārkaḷ. viṉaippayaṉē piṟavikkuk kāraṇameṉṟum, vētākamap poruḷkaḷ meyp poruḷkaḷeṉṟum kaṭavuḷoruvaruḷa reṉṟum, nampi oppuk koḷḷātavarkaḷai mikavum ikaḻntu aruvaruttu yāṇṭum puṟakkaṇippar. celvaḻikaḷil civālayaṅkaḷ kaṇṇetirp paṭiṉ, aracar tāmum vākaṉamiḻintu taṅkaḷ veṇkoṟṟak kuṭaikaḷaic cāyttu vaṇaṅkic celvārkaḷ. aracarkaḷ tamporuṭṭu māttiramaṉṟit tam kuṭimakkaḷiṉ nalaṅkaḷaik kuṟittum aṭikkaṭi paṟpala vēḷvikaḷai yum yākaṅkaḷaiyuñ ceyvārkaḷ. aṅṅaṉañ ceytuḻi antaṇar pāvalarkaḷ mutalōrum pala ticai maṉṉarkaḷuṅ kūṭik kuḻumik kaḷip peytuvar. īkaiyē tarumaṅkaḷellāvaṟṟiṉum mikac ciṟantateṉṟu yāṇṭum pāvalar viyantu kūṟuvar. ceykuṉṟiyaṟṟik kaḻaṅkāṭip pōtukoytu puṉal viḷaiyāṭip poḻutu pōkkuvar. āṭavaraip pōṟ peṇkaḷuṅ kaḷḷaruntik kaḷikūrvar. kaḷḷil kāramēṟumpaṭi ataṉai vēyṅkuḻal mutaliyavaṟṟilaṭaittum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṟ peytaṭakkiyum vaippārkaḷ.” (tamiḻ varalāṟu pak.83-84)
23. poḻutu pōkku
1.peṇkaḷ poḻutupōkku
peṇkaḷukku vīṭṭil kiḷip piḷḷaikaḷaip pēcap paḻakkutal, vaḷḷai ammāṉai eṉṉum pāṭṭukaḷaip pāṭutal, kaḻaṅku aṉṟēl pantu viḷaiyāṭutal mutaliyaṉa ciṟanta poḻutu pōkkukaḷākum.
“kantāṭu māliyāṉaik kārvaṇṇaṉ pāvai
karumēkak kuḻaṉmaṭavār kaicōrntu niṟpak
kontāṭum pūṅkuḻaluṅ kōtaikaḷu māṭak
koypolan tukilacaitta koycakantāḻn tāṭa
vantāṭun tēṉumural varivaṇṭu māṭa
maṇivaṭamum poṉñāṇum vārmulaimē lāṭap
pantāṭu māṭētaṉ paṭaineṭuṅka ṇāṭap
paṇaimeṉṟō ṇiṉṟāṭap pantāṭu kiṉṟāḷ.” (cūḷāmaṇi-cuyamvaram)
mālaik kālattil celvarkaḷ kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭa tēr kaḷai vītivaḻiyē celuttic celvar. upparikaikaḷiṉiṉṟu veḷiyē nōkkum avar makaḷiriṭattiruntum varum naṟumaṇam vītikaḷil vīcum. aḻakāka uṭuttiya vālipar aḻakiya āparaṇaṅkaḷaiyum pūntāraiyum aṇinta parattaiyaruṭaṉ vītivaḻiyē ulāvap pōvar. āṭavar parattai yaruṭaṉ naṭpuk kōṭal iḻivākak karutappaṭavillai. ‘parattaiyiṟ pirital ellārkku murittē’ eṉpar tolkāppiyar. cēval kavutāri āṭṭukkiṭāy mutaliyavaṟṟaip pōrkku viṭutalum akkālap poḻutu pōkkukaḷākum.
2. ciṟuvariṉ poḻutupōkku
ciṟār, taccarāṟ ceyyappaṭṭuc ciṟiya kutiraikaḷāliḻukkappaṭum tērkaḷiṟ cellum iṉpattai eytārāyiṉum tam kaikaḷiṉāl viḷaiyāṭṭuttērai iḻuttu iṉpuṟuvar.
“taccaṉ ceyta ciṟumā vaiya
mūrntiṉ puṟāa rāyiṉuṅ kaiyi
ṉīrttiṉ puṟūu miḷaiyōr” (kuṟun-61)
ciṟiya peṇkaḷ kaṭalilē nīrāṭumpōtu maṇalilē ciṟu vīṭukaḷ iḻaittu viḷaiyāṭuvar. (kuṟun. 326) vayira ulakkaiyiṉāl avaliṭikkum peṇkaḷ ulakkaiyai eṟintuviṭṭu vaṇṭalil viḷaiyāṭuvārkaḷ. (kuṟun. 238)
ūñcal (uñcāl) āṭuvatum ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ poḻutu pōkkākum.
“peruṅkayiṟu nālu mirumpaṉam piṇaiyaṟ
pūṅka ṇāya mūkka.” (naṟ-90)
paḷḷikkup pōkāta ciṟār, vēppa nīḻalil kōṭukaḷ kīṟi nellikkāykoṇṭu kaḻaṅkāṭuvārkaḷ.
“vēmpiṉ puḷḷi nīḻaṟ
kaṭṭaḷai yaṉṉa vaṭṭaraṅ kiḻaittuk
kallāc ciṟāar nelli vaṭ ṭāṭum.” (naṟ-3)
peṇkaḷ vīṭṭiṉ muṟṟattiluḷḷa
maṇalil kaḻaṅkāṭuvārkaḷ (naṟ-79)
ciṟār kuḷaṅkaḷil kutittu āḻntu kal maṇ mutaliyavaṟṟai eṭuttu viḷaiyāṭuvārkaḷ. tāyam āṭutal perumpālār poḻutu pōkkākum. “īraintu peṟṟāṉ uḷḷam pōl” (kali)
24. peṇkaḷ kalvi
tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil pala pāṭalkaḷaic ceytavarkaḷ peṇ pulavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. āṭavaraip pōlap peṇkaḷum kalviyiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. pōrkkōlaṅkoṇṭu cellum paṭaikaḷilum, ‘viṟaliyar’ eṉṉum peṇkaḷ iruntāreṉil peṇkaḷ kalvi nilai mika ēṟṟam peṟṟiruntateṉṟē colla vēṇṭiyiruk kiṉṟatu.
kāvaṟ peṇṭu, pēymakaḷ iḷaveyiṉi, mācāttiyār, pūṅkaṇut tiraiyār, poṉmuṭiyār, oḷavaiyār mutaliyōr puṟanāṉūṟṟil cila pākkaḷaic ceytavarkaḷāvar.
25. tamiḻariṉ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ
1. tāli tarittal
paḻaiya nāḷiṟ kuṟiñci nilattu iḷaiñar puliyai eytukoṉṟu tamatu vīracceyalaik kāṇpittu malaivāṇar makaḷirai vatuvai ceyvar. puliyaikkoṉṟa āṭavaṉ taṉatu vīrattukku aṭaiyāḷamākap puliyiṉ paṟkaḷai nāṇiṟ kōttup peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil aṇivāṉ. ivvaḻakkamē nāḷaṭaivil tāli tarikkum vaḻakkamāka vantatu. itu tamiḻnāṭṭukkē uriyatu.
2. maṉaikaḷ
vaṟiyavar vīṭukaḷ maṇṇiṉāl eṭukkappaṭṭa cuvarkaḷuṭaiyaṉa; pulliṉāl allatu teṉṉaṅ kīṟṟukaḷiṉāl vēyappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭṭiṉ muṉpuṟattil pantar irukkum. ataṉait talaivāyil eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar. cuvarkaḷ civanta maṇṇiṉāl meḻukappaṭṭiruntaṉa. paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷil vīṭukaḷ ceṅkalliṉāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭu ōṭṭiṉāl vēyappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ māṉiṉ kaṇpōṉṟa cāḷaraṅkaḷ veḷiccam pukuvataṟkāka amaikkappaṭṭaṉa. cila kōṭṭai kottaḷaṅkaḷ ceṅkalliṉālum cuṇṇāmpiṉālum eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa.
“pulvēy kurampai’ “cilkāṟṟicaikkum palpukai nalil” “ilai vēy kurampai” eṉap pattup pāṭṭil collappaṭuvatai nōkkuka.
3. poṟikaḷ
entirac ceykaikaḷāl māṭcimaippaṭṭa ūrtikaḷum piṟavum iruntaṉa. kār nāṟpatu 21 ām pāṭṭil ‘poṟimāṇ puṉaitiṇṭēr’ eṉak kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka. itaṉāl kutirai mutaliyavaṟṟāl iḻukkap paṭātu iyantirattiṉāl iḻukkappaṭum tēr iruntateṉa viḷaṅkukiṟatu. poṟikaḷāl nīr vaṟṟavum niṟaiyavum ceyyappaṭum kiṇaṟukaḷum vāvikaḷumiruntaṉa. avai, ēntirak kiṇaṟu’ ‘ēntiravāvi’ eṉṉum peyarkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. peruṅkataiyil pala poṟikaḷiṉ vivaram kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. avaikaḷāvaṉa: “kāḷaikaḷiṉṟi viraintu cellum ēntiravaṇṭiyum pala vīrarkaḷai āyutaṅkaḷuṭaṉ taṉṉuḷḷaṭakkik koṇṭu uyiruḷḷatu pōl naṭantu ceṉṟu mayakkum yāṉaippoṟiyum, ēṟa virumpiyavarkaḷait taṉṉuḷ ēṟṟikkoṇṭu pārkka vēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷai avarkaḷ pārkkumpaṭi ākāya vaḻiyē cellum vimāṉamum kālattaik kāṭṭum entiramum, kaṭikaiyāramum (kaṭikārameṉa ikkālam vaḻaṅkum), kaḻuttil mālaiyāyaṇitaliṉ ārameṉappaṭṭatu. - (kaṭikai-āram)nāṇmīṉ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ tōṟṟattaiyum attamittalaiyum pulappaṭuttum poṟimaṇṭilattai uḷḷē peṟṟu aracamaṅkaiyar ēṟutaṟkurittāyirunta vaṇṭiyum piṭikaiyum (ippōtuḷḷa īruruḷi Bicycle pōṉṟatu) iṉṉum ivai pōṉṟa pala vicittirap poruṭ kaḷumām”
4. paṭukkai
“ciṅkak kāṉmēl taitta tūṅku kaṭṭiliṉ ilaṅkum māṭcimai viḷaṅkum aṉṉattūviyāṟ ceyta melliya paṭukkai” eṉak kalit tokai 13 il oru talaivaṉiṉ paṭukkaiyiṉ viparaṅ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. neṭunalvāṭaiyil yāṉaik kompiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatum, nāṭā viṉāṟ piṉṉappaṭṭatum palvēṟu mettaikaḷ pōṭṭup pūkkaḷiṉitaḻ parappit tukiliṉ tūmaṭi virikkappaṭṭatumākiya kaṭṭil eṉṟum, kaṭṭi liṉ mēl vitāṉattil tiṅkaḷum urōkiṇiyum eḻutappaṭṭiruntaṉa veṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
“puli vaṭivamainta kālaiyuṭaiya kaṭṭilil elimayirāṟ ceyyap paṭṭa pūttoḻilaiyuṭaiya pōrvaiyai virittuk kappalilē vanta palavakaiyāṉa paṭukkaikaḷil ārāyntu paṭuttu” eṉap peruṅkataiyil kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivai celvarkaḷukkuriya paṭukkaikaḷākum;
āyar taṭikaḷiṉāl varintu kaṭṭappaṭṭa kaṭṭilil āṭṭut tōlaip pāyākak koṇṭu uṟaṅkuvārkaḷeṉṟum, eyiṟṟiyar māṉṟōlaip paṭukkaiyākak koḷvārkaḷeṉṟum ilakkiyattiṟ collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
5. viḷakkukaḷ
vaṟiṟōr maṇṇiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa viḷakkukaḷaip payaṉpaṭut tiṉar. ēṉaiyōr irumpu cempu veṇkalam mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ ceyyap paṭṭa viḷakkukakaiḷ payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. yavaṉarkaḷāṟ koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa aṉṉa viḷakku, pāvai viḷakku mutaliyaṉavum upayōkikkappaṭṭaṉa.
“yavaṉa rōtima viḷakku” (perumpāṇ)
“pāvai viḷakkiṟ parūuccuṭar aḻala” (mullaippāṭṭu)
“yavaṉap pāvai aṇi viḷakku” (peruṅkatai)
6. paṭṭaṅkaḷ
ikkālam aracarāl aḷikkappaṭum paṭṭaṅkaḷaip pōl tamiḻ aracar pōr vīrarukkum piṟarukkum pala paṭṭaṅkaḷai aḷittaṉar. eṭṭippaṭṭam-itu eṭṭip pūvaippōl poṉṉāl ceytu vaṇikarukkuk koṭukkum paṭṭam. ēṉātip paṭṭam-cēṉāpatiyar mutalāyiṉōrkku aracaṉāl aḷikkappaṭum paṭṭam. kāvitip paṭṭam-uḻuvittuṇpōrkaḷiṟ ciṟantōr aracaṉāṟ peṟum paṭṭam. ēṉātip paṭṭattukku mōtiramaḷittal vaḻakku. “ēṉāti mōtirañ ceṟikkun tiruvuṭaiyāṉoruvaṉ” eṉpatu iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷurai.
7. veḷḷaṇi nāḷ
aracar tām piṟanta nāḷil veḷḷaṇi aṇintu koṇṭāṭuvar. avar muṭicūṭṭum nāḷaiyum perunāḷākak koṇṭāṭutal marapu. innāḷil aracaṉ ciṟaikkōṭṭattuḷḷōrai viṭutalai ceyvāṉ.
8. cila paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ
pāṇṭiyanāṭu muttukkuppērpōṉatu. ‘mārkō pōlō’ eṉpavar muttuk kuḷippaip paṟṟik kūṟiyavaralāṟu uṇmaiyāyirukkiṟatu. “cuntarapāṇṭi tēvaruṭaiya nāṭṭil mutaṟṟaramāṉa periya muttukkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai eppaṭi eṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpataip paṟṟic collukiṟēṉ. muttukkuḷippōr peritum ciṟitumākiya marakkalaṅ kaḷaik koṇṭu ilaṅkait tīvukkum intiyāvukkum iṭaiyiluḷḷa kuṭāvukkuc celkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ mutalil pettilār (Bettlar) eṉṉum ōr iṭattai aṭaikiṟārkaḷ. piṉpu kuṭāvukkuḷ aṟupatu mail tūram celkiṉṟaṉar. iṅkē avarkaḷ tōṇikaḷai naṅkūramiṭṭuc ciṟiya vaḷḷaṅkaḷil ēṟikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. aṅkē vaṇikarkaḷ pala kūṭṭaṅ kaḷākap pirintu celkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ muttuk kuḷippavarkaḷaic cittirai mātan toṭaṅki vaikāci mātam varaikkum campaḷattukku amarttikkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. aṅkuk kiṭaikkum muttukkaḷil patti loṉṟai aracaṉuṭaiya kaṭamaiyākak koṭuttuviṭal vēṇṭum: muttuk kuḷippōraip periya mīṉkaḷ tuṉpañ ceyyātiruttaṟ poruṭṭu avaṟṟai mantirattāl kaṭṭukiṉṟavarkaḷukku irupatil oṉṟu koṭukka vēṇṭum. maccaṅkaḷai mantiraṅkaḷāṟ kaṭṭupavarkaḷ pirāmaṇarkaḷāvar. avarkaḷuṭaiya mantira vali pakal nērattil māttiram palikkum. iravilē maccaṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ eṇṇappaṭi tiriyum poruṭṭu mantirattai aviḻttu viṭukiṟārkaḷ. muttukkuḷippōr ciṟiya vaḷḷaṅkaḷil ēṟiyavuṭaṉ nālu mutal paṉṉiraṇṭu pākat taṇṇīruḷ kutittuc cuḻiyōṭi aṭiyiṟ ceṉṟu mūccaṭakkakkūṭiya varaiyil taṅkukiṟārkaḷ. kīḻē kāṇappaṭum muttuc cippikaḷaip poṟukki araiyilē kaṭṭiyirukkum valai pōṉṟa oru paikkuṭpōṭṭukkoṇṭu mēlē vantu maṟupaṭiyum kīḻē celkiṉṟārkaḷ. ivvakaiyākap peruntokaiyāṉa cippikaḷ eṭukkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.1 iccippikaḷiliruntu ulakam muḻumaikkum celkiṉṟa muttukkaḷ eṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. muttukkuḷippiṉāl aracaṉ perun tokaiyāṉa varuvāyaṭaikiṟāṉ. arai cākkiyō (Saggio) niṟaikku mēṟpaṭṭa muttukkaḷai oruvarāvatu avaratu tēcattukkappāl koṇṭu pōtal kūṭātu. avvakaiyāṉa muttukkaḷait tāṉ peṟa virumpiyē aracaṉ avvakaiyāṉa caṭṭattai uṇṭākkiyirukkiṟāṉ. avaṉiṭatti luḷḷa muttukkaḷ eṇṇikkaiyillātaṉa. periya muttukkaḷ allatu irattiṉaṅkaḷ vaittiruppavarkaḷ avaṟṟait taṉṉiṭam koṇṭu vantāl atu peṟum vilaiyil iru maṭaṅkutāṉ koṭuppatāka āṇṭuk korumuṟai nāṭu muḻumaiyum aṟivikkiṟāṉ. avvakaiyāṉa muttukkaḷ irattiṉaṅkaḷ vaittiruppōr avaṟṟai aracaṉiṭattil koṭuttu vilaiyaip peṟṟukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar.
muttukkaḷait tavira vēṟu vilaiyuyarnta poruḷkaḷaiyum aracaṉ vaittirukkiṟāṉ. avaṉ palavakai irattiṉaṅkaḷ patitta āraṅ kaḷaik kaḻuttil aṇintirukkiṟāṉ. 104 periya muttukkaḷum irattiṉaṅ kaḷum cērkkappaṭṭa oru paṭṭunūl avaṉ mārpilē toṅkukiṉṟatu. avaṉ kālaiyilum mālaiyilum 104 tutikaḷaik kaṭavuḷukkuc colla vēṇṭiyiruppatāl iṅṅaṉam aṇintirukkiṟāṉeṉṟu collappaṭu kiṉṟatu. ivvakaiyākavē ivaṉ muṉṉōrum ceytu vantārkaḷ. muṉṉōr vaittirunta ārattaiyē ivaṉ peṟṟāṉ.
muttukkaḷ aḻuttic ceyyappaṭṭa mūṉṟu poṉ vaḷaiyalkaḷai aracaṉ tōḷilē pūṇṭirukkiṟāṉ. avai pōṉṟa vaḷaiyalkaḷaik kālilum aṇintirukkiṉṟāṉ. kālviralil avai pōṉṟa mōtiraṅ kaḷaiyumaṇintirukkiṉṟāṉ.
iṅṅaṉam aracaṉ aṇintirukkum aṇikaḷiṉ vilai oru nāṭṭiṉ varuvāyilum atikamākum. ivvakaiyāṉa poruḷkaḷ avaṉiṭattiṟ pala uṇṭu.
avaṉiṟakkumpōtu avaṉuṭaiya piḷḷaikaḷ kaḷañciyattait tīṇṭamāṭṭārkaḷ. ‘eṅkaḷ tantai evvaḷavu cēmittu vaittiruk kiṟārō avvaḷavu nāṅkaḷum tēṭi vaittarukka vēṇṭu’ meṉac collukiṟārkaḷ. vāciv (Wassif) eṉṉum makamatiya caritāciriyar kūṟuvati liruntu mārkkō pōlō pāṇṭiyarkaḷ tiraṭṭi vaittirunta poruḷ nilaiyaic cariyāka aṟintiruntāṉeṉpatu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. “kulacēkara ṉatu karuvūlam vilaimatippuḷḷa poruḷ niṟaintatu. avaṉatu karuvūlam 12,000 kōṭi uḷḷatāyiruntatu. itaiviṭa muttu pavaḻam irattiṉam mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷ iruntaṉa. avaṉuṭaiya celvattai aḷaviṭṭuk kūṟa moḻi iṭaṅkoṭātu” eṉa avaṉ kūṟiyuḷḷār. mārkkōpōlō, aracaṉaiyum avaṉatu capaiyaiyuṅkuṟittuk kēṭpataṟkiṉpamākiya ceytikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. “inta aracaṉ aiññūṟu maṉaiviyarai uṭaiyava ṉāyirukkiṟāṉ. avaṉukkup pala piḷḷaikaḷuṇṭu. aracaṉaic cūḻntu pala pirapukkaḷ irukkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ avaṉuṭaṉkūṭac cavāriceyki ṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ eppoḻutum aracaṉukkup pakkattil irukkiṟārkaḷ. aracāṅkattil avarkaḷukkup periya atikāramuṇṭu. avarkaḷ ‘āpat tukku utavikaḷ’ eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭuvārkaḷ. aracaṉ iṟantu eriyil koḷuttappaṭumpōtu av vāpattukkutavikaḷum pirētattaic cūḻntu tīyil viḻuntu iṟantu pōkiṉṟārkaḷ. immaiyil aracaṉukkutaviyā yirunta tām maṟumaiyilum avaṉukku utaviyāyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu avarkaḷ collik koḷkiṉṟaṉar.
mārkkōpōlō potumakkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaiyaik kuṟittuk kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. “avarkaḷuṭaiya naṭai uṭai mutaliyaṉa avaṉai āccariyamuṟac ceytaṉa. ‘māpār’1 eṉṉum mākāṇattiluḷḷavarkaḷ araikku mēl niruvāṇikaḷākavēyiruntaṉar. ataṉāl aṅkē taiyaṟkārarkaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai. aracaṉ mutal vaṟiyavarkaḷ īṟāka ellōrum araiyil ōr āṭaimāttiram tarittukkoḷvar.”
akkālattil (cati) uṭaṉkaṭṭai ēṟum vaḻakkam iruntateṉṟum kolaikkut tīrkkappaṭṭa kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ tamatu viruppattiṉpaṭi yātēṉumoru kōyilukkut tammaip paliyākak koṭuttuviṭalā meṉṟa piramāṇamiruntateṉṟum, caṉaṅkaḷ pacukkaḷai vaṇaṅkiṉārka ḷeṉṟum, māṭṭu māmicam akkālattiṟ pucikkappaṭavillaiyeṉṟum mārkkōpōlō kūṟukiṉṟāṉ.
innāṭṭilē uḷḷavarkaḷ vīṭukaḷaic cāṇiyāl meḻuku vārkaḷ. periyavarkaḷum ciṟiyavarkaḷumākiya ellōrum taraiyilēyē iruppārkaḷ.
innāṭṭavar uṭampai maṟaittuk koḷḷātu caṇṭaikkuc celkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ īṭṭiyum kēṭayamum vaittiruppārkaḷ.
āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum nāḷum irumuṟai kuḷippārkaḷ. appaṭic ceyyātavarkaḷ tāḻntavarkaḷākak karutappaṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ valakkaiyāl uṇavai eṭuttu uṇpatēyaṉṟi, iṭakkaiyāl tīṇṭamāṭṭārkaḷ. ivvāṟē nīr parukukiṟārkaḷ. ovvoruvarum taṉittaṉi ovvoru pāttiram vaittirukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ nīr aruntumpōtu pāttiram utaṭṭil muṭṭuvatillai. ekkāraṇattiṉā lāvatu avarkaḷ nīraruntum pāttirattai utaṭṭil muṭṭaviṭa māṭṭārkaḷ. aṉṉiyaṉoruvaṉ nīraruntap pāttiram vaittirā viṭṭāl avaṉ uṇṇumpaṭi nīraik kaiyil ūṟṟuvārkaḷ.
avarkaḷ kuṟṟavāḷikaḷukkut taṇṭaṉai iṟuppatil mikavum kavaṉam celuttiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ matu uṇpatillai. matupāṉañ ceypavarkaḷ nampikkai nāṇayam uṭaiyavarākak karutappaṭamāṭ ṭārkaḷ eṉṟu caṭṭañ ceytiruntārkaḷ. kaṭaṉ koṭuttavaṉ kaṭaṉ kāraṉaip palamuṟaiyum paṇattaik kēṭka. avaṉ koṭāviṭṭāl, allatu pala keṭukkaḷ coṉṉāl avaṉ oru vaṭṭaṅ kīṟik kaṭaṉ kāraṉai ataṟkuḷ niṟuttalām. kaṭaṉ paṭṭavaṉ kaṭaṉ koṭuttavaṉait tiruptip paṭuttiyapiṉ allatu avaṉukkut takunta poṟuppuc ceyta piṉtāṉ avvaṭṭattiṉiṉṟum vilakalām. avaṉ ivvāṟu ceyyātu vaṭṭattiṉ veḷiyē celvāṉāyiṉ, avaṉukkuk kolait taṇṭaṉai vitikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
avarkaḷiṉ cāmuttirikkā ilaṭcaṇam aṟintavarkaḷ palar uḷar. cakuṉam pārppatil avarkaḷukku mikunta nampikkai. piḷḷai piṟantavuṭaṉ atu piṟanta mātam tēti nāḻikai mutaliyavaṟṟai eḻutivaikkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ceyyum karumam ovvoṉṟum cōtiṭa campantamā yiruppatālēyē iṅṅaṉam ceykiṟārkaḷ.
“inta nakarattiluḷḷa caṉaṅkaḷum intiyāviṉ maṟu iṭaṅkaḷi luḷḷavarkaḷum ‘tāmpūlam’ eṉṉum oru vakai ilaiyai eppoḻutum vāykkuḷ vaittu mellukiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ avvilaiyai meṉṟu umiḻ nīrai veḷiyē tuppiviṭukiṉṟaṉar. aracarkaḷum celvarkaḷum karuppūram mutaliyaṉa kalanta vācaṉaip poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ ataṉaip payaṉpaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ. ivvaḻakkam nalattukkum mikavum nallateṉac collappaṭukiṟatu.
iṅkuk kutiraikaḷ kiṭaippatillai. itaṉāl piṟa nāṭukaḷiliruntu kutiraikaḷ vāṅkuvatil innāṭu peruntokaip paṇattaic cela viṭṭatu. kis (Kis), hōms (Hormes), ṭōvar (Dofar), cōēr (Soer), eṭiṉ (Aden) mutaliya nāṭṭavarkaḷ inta nāṭṭu aracaṉukkum ava ṉuṭaiya nālu cakōtararukkum viṟkumpaṭi kutiraikaḷ koṇṭu varu vārkaḷ. oru kutiraiyiṉ vilai 500 cakki (Saggi) poṉṉākum. atu 100 veḷḷi mārks (Marks)kkuc cari.
‘vāciv’piṉ varumāṟu kūṟukiṟār:
kutiraikaḷ iṅku vantatum paccaivāḷi koṭuppataṟkup patil vaṟutta vāḷiyaiyum, tāṉiyaṅkaḷaiyum neyyuṭaṉ kalantu koṭuk kiṟārkaḷ. kuṭippataṟkuk kāycciya pacuviṉ pālaik koṭukkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ 40 nāṭkaḷukku avaikaḷaik kayiṟukaḷāl pantiyiṟ kaṭṭik koḻukkumpaṭi viṭukiṟārkaḷ. intiyap pōrvīrar avaṟṟiṉmītu tuṭṭa tēvataikaḷaip pōla ēṟic cavāri ceykiṟārkaḷ. ciṟitu kālattukkup piṉ vēkamuḷḷa kutiraikaḷ palamaṟṟaṉavāy upayōkamaṟṟup pōkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl āṇṭutōṟum putiya kutiraikaḷ vāṅka nērkiṉṟatu.
mārkkōpōlō ki.pi. 1254 mutal 1324 varaiyil vāḻntavaṉ.
perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭai
(itil paḻantamiḻariṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ naṉku kūṟappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa)
iḷantiraiyaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu paricil peṟṟāṉoruvaṉ. paricil peṟutaṟku virumpiya perumpāṇaṉoruvaṉai etirppaṭṭu avvaracaṉ pāl ēkumāṟu avaṉai āṟṟuppaṭutti iḷantiraiyaṉiṉ irācatāṉi yākiya kāñcipurattiṟkuc cellum mārkkattiṉaik kūṟukiṉṟāṉ.
“avaṉuṭaiya nakaril vaḻippōkkar alaṟumpaṭi veṭṭi avar poruḷkaḷaik koḷḷai koḷḷum tīyōrillai. avaṉuṭaiya kāṭṭil iṭiyēṟum iṭiyātu; pāmpukaḷum tīṇṭā; puli mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷum tīṅkiḻaiyā, avvakaiyāṉa kāṭṭakattē nī payamiṉṟi iḷaippāṟic cellalām. akkāṭṭil kavarpaṭṭa pala vaḻikaḷ uṇṭu. avvaḻikaḷil umaṇar uppēṟṟiya vaṇṭikaḷai ōṭṭic celvar. vaṇṭiyiṉ accu mattaḷam pōṉṟatu. ataṉ irumuṉaikaḷilum cillukaḷ māṭṭap peṟṟirukkum. acciṉ ūṭē ēṇikkālkaḷ pōṉṟa iraṇṭu maraṅkaḷ māṭṭappeṟṟirukkum. ammaraṅkaḷil nāṭṭappaṭṭa taṭikaḷiṉ mēl kuṭilpōl pāyiṉāl vēyappaṭṭirukkum. vaṇṭikaḷiṉ muṉpuṟattē kōḻikkūṭukaḷ vaikkappaṭṭirukkum. ural pōṉṟa uṟikaḷ vaṇṭikaḷiṉ muṉ puṟattē uyarak kaṭṭappaṭṭu avai mītu nellikkāy, puḷiyaṅkāy mutaliyaṉa ūṟaviṭṭa pāṉaikaḷ vaikkappaṭṭirukkum.
umaṇappeṇ kaiyilē vēppilaiyaiyum piḷḷaiyaiyum uṭaiyavaḷāy vaṇṭiyiṉ muṉpuṟattēyiruntu erutai mutukilē aṭit tōṭṭuvāḷ. taḻaiyiṉāṟ kaṭṭiya mālaiyai aṇinta umaṇar, pala ciṟu tuḷaikaḷaiyuṭaiya nukattilē kaṭṭappaṭṭa erutukaḷ vaṇṭiyait tirukāmalum accu muṟiyāmalum pakkattē kāttuk koṇṭum kaḷaittāṟ pūṭṭuvataṟkup pala erutukaḷai ōṭṭikkoṇṭum uppiṉ vilaiyaik kūṟikkoṇṭum kavarpaṭṭa vaḻikaḷāṟ celvar.
piṟanāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum vanta vaṇikar māṇikkam, muttu, cantaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟai eṅkum tirintu viṟpārkaḷ. avarkaḷuṭaiya mārpukaḷ ampukaḷāṟ kiḻikkappaṭṭu āṟiya taḻumpukaḷuṭaiyaṉa. tammai etirppōrait tākkuvataṟkuk kaiyilē vēl piṭittiruppar; yāṉait tantattāl kaṭainteṭutta uṟaiyilē iṭṭavāḷai varikaḷaiyuṭaiya cīlaiyilē kōttut tōḷiṉ oru pakkattē toṅkaviṭṭuc caṭṭai yiṭṭuc cerupputtoṭṭu otta kaṉamākac cērnta miḷakup potikaḷaik kavarpaṭṭa vaḻikaḷil cumantu cellum kaḻutaikaḷiṉ piṉ celvārkaḷ. ivvāṟu cakaṭaṅkaḷum, kaḻutaikaḷum celkiṉṟa kavarpaṭṭa vaḻikaḷ cantikkiṉṟa iṭattil aracaṉāl niyamikkappaṭṭa cuṅkaṅ koḷvōr viṟpaṭaiyuṭaṉ kāttiruppārkaḷ.
akkāṭṭilē eyiṉarkaḷuṭaiya ciṟu kuṭicaikaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. avai aṇilum eliyum nuḻaiyātapaṭi īntiṉ ōlaiyiṉāl vēyappaṭṭu muṭpaṉṟiyiṉ mutukupōṉṟa puṟattiṉaiyuṭaiyaṉavā yirukkum.
piḷḷaiyaip peṟṟa eyiṟṟiyar akkuṭicaikaḷiṉ orupuṟattē māṉṟōlkaḷil muṭaṅkik kiṭakka, maṟṟavarkaḷ pāraiyiṉāl karampai nilattaik kiḷaṟi, eṟumpu cērttu vaitta pullariciyai eṭuttuvantu 1pārvaimāṉ kaṭṭappaṭṭa viḷaviṉ niḻalaiyuṭaiya muṟṟattilē tōṇṭappaṭṭa nila uralilē peytu vayira ulakkaiyiṉāl kuṟṟi, āḻnta kiṇaṟṟil uvar nīrai moṇṭu vantu, paḻaiya oṟuvāyp pōṉa pāṉaiyilē ulaiyai vārttu, muṟinta aṭuppilē vaittu ākku vārkaḷ. avar kuṭicaikaḷai aṭaintāl ivvāṟu camaikkappaṭṭa cōṟṟai uppuk kaṇṭattuṭaṉē tēkkilaiyil iṭṭu uṇṇum paṭi koṭuppārkaḷ.
ikkuṭicaikaḷaik kaṭantu ceṉṟāl eyiṉar araṇaik kāṇa lām. aṅku matilkaḷaiyuṭaiyaṉavum ūkampulliṉāl vēyappaṭṭaṉavu mākiya vīṭukaḷ uṇṭu. avaṟṟuḷ pakaivaraik kuttiyatāl kūr maḻuṅkiya vēlkaḷ maṇikaṭṭiṉa paricaikaḷōṭu viṟkaḷiṟ cāttappeṟṟirukkum talaivāyiliṉ tiraṇṭa kālkaḷil ampuk kaṭṭukaḷum tuṭiyum tūṅkum. caṅkiliyilē kaṭṭappaṭṭa nāykaḷ vīṭṭaik kāvalkāttu niṟkum. vīṭṭaic cuṟṟi muḷvēliyum ataṉaic cūḻntu kāvaṟkāṭum kāṇap paṭum. vāyiliṉ uṭkatavukaḷ tiraṇṭa maraṅkaḷāl tāḷ pōṭappaṭ ṭirukkum. ivvakaiyāṉa eyiṉar araṇkaḷil taṅkiṉāl, nāykaḷ kaṭittuk koṇṭuvanta caṅkumaṇi pōṉṟa muṭṭaikaḷaiyuṭaiya uṭumpiṉ poriyalālē maṟaikkappaṭṭa cōṟṟai vīṭukaṭōṟum peṟuvāy.
appāṟ ceṉṟāl kuṟiñci nilattai aṭaivāy. aṅkē vāṭṭoḻilaic ceyyum kuṭiyiṟ piṟanta puliyiṉ mīcaiyaip pōṉṟa tāṭiyaiyuṭaiya kuṟavar talaivaṉ. koṭiya villaiyuṭaiya kāvala ruṭaṉē kāvalaiyuṭaiya pakaivaratu mullainilattai aṭaivāṉ. viṭiyaṟ kālaiyilē avarkaḷ pacukkaḷai ōṭṭikkoṇṭupōyk kaḷḷukku vilaiyākak koṭuppāṉ. piṉ taṉatu vīṭṭilē nellāṟ ceyta kaḷḷai uṇṭu, āṭṭuk kiṭāyai muṟṟattil aṟuttu uṇṭu, mattaḷaṅ koṭṭa naṭuvē niṉṟu, iṭattōḷai valappakkattē vaḷaittuk koṇṭu pakaṟpoḻutilē makiḻntu āṭuvāṉ. ivvakaiyiṉatākiya kuṟiñci nilattaik kaṭantapiṉpu mullai nilattai aṭaivīrkaḷ.
aṅkē vīṭukaḷ kuṟukiya kālkaḷiṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. akkuṟukiya kālkaḷil āṭṭumantaikaḷ tiṉpataṟkut taḻaikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. vīṭṭiṉ vāyilkaḷil ciṟiya paṟṟaikaḷ uṇṭu. katavukaḷ kayiṟṟiṉāl variyappaṭṭaṉa. kayiṟṟiṉāl variyappaṭṭu varaku vaikkōl parappappaṭṭa paṭukkaiyil kiṭāyiṉ tōlaip paṭukkaiyākavuṭaiya mutiyōṉ kāvalākat tuyilum pala kuṭicaikaḷ aṅkuk kāṇappaṭum. muṟṟattilē aṟaiyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟa muḷaikaḷil tāmaṇikaḷaiyuṭaiya kayiṟukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. cīḻkkai aṭikkaiyiṉālē iṭaiyar maṭitta vāyaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷāyiruppārkaḷ.
viṭiyaṟkālattilē māmai niṟattaiyum tāḷuruvi acaikiṉṟa kātiṉaiyumuṭaiya āymakaḷ mattu āravārikkumpaṭi tayiraik kaṭaintu veṇṇeyai eṭuppāḷ. piṉpu puḷḷiyākat tayirteṟitta mōrp pāṉaiyaip pūvāṟ ceyta cummāṭṭiṉmēl vaittu mōrai viṟpāḷ; viṟṟatiṉāṟ kiṭaitta nel mutaliyavaṟṟaik koṇṭupōyt taṉ cuṟṟattārai uṇṇappaṇṇuvāḷ; avaḷ tāṉ neyyai viṟṟa vilaikkuk kaṭṭiyākiya pacumpoṉṉai vāṅkāḷāyp pālerumaiyai yum, nalla pacuviṉaiyum, erumainākiṉaiyum koḷḷuvāḷ; iv vakai yiṉatākiya āyarkuṭiyiruppukaḷil taṅkuvāyāyiṉ, tiṉaiyariciyālaṭṭa cilutta cōṟṟaip pāluṭaṉē peṟuvāy.
appāl mullai nilattu ūrkaḷai aṭaivīrkaḷ. aṅku vīṭu kaḷiṉ muṟṟattil varaku mutaliyaṉa pōṭṭu vaikkum kūṭukaḷ (kutir) kāṇappaṭum. varaku tirikaikaḷ talaivāyilil naṭappaṭṭirukkum. koṭṭil vīṭukaḷ varaku vaikkōliṉāl vēyappaṭṭirukkum. av vīṭukaḷiṉ oru puṟam aṭupperittatiṉāl pukai cūḻntirukkum. vīṭukaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷil vaṇṭic cakkaraṅkaḷum kalappaikaḷum cārtti vaikkappaṭ ṭirukkum. aṅkē taṅkiṉāl varaku cōṟṟaip peṟuvāy.
mullai nilattaic cārnta marutattil vīṭu niṟainta uṇaviṉai yuṭaiya uḻavar vayalkaḷai vitaippārkaḷ. aṟukkum paruvattil ataṉ kaṇ taṅkum kuṟumpūḻ (kāṭai) paṟakkalāṟṟāta iḷaiya piḷḷaikaḷaik kūṭṭik koṇṭu mullai nilattē cellum. vayalkaḷiluḷḷa tēṉāṟu kiṉṟa pūvai mālaiyākak kaṭṭic cūṭi, ataṉai veṟutta toḻil cey vāruṭaiya piḷḷaikaḷ muḷḷiyiṉ kariya pūvaip paṟittuk kōraiyaip pallālē meṉṟu kiḻitta nārāṟ kaṭṭiya mālaiyai īruṭaiya talaiyilē yaṇintu kaṭampiṉ tātai mārpilē appuvārkaḷ. avarkaḷ paḻañcōṟṟai veṟuttup paṉṟi mutaliya kāttaṟku varampilē kaṭṭap paṭṭa putiya vaikkōlāl vēynta ciṟiya kuṭiliṉ muṟṟattē avaliṭip pārkaḷ. avaliṭikkum ulakkaiyiṉōcai kiḷḷaikaḷai veruvac ceyyum. vaḷainta katiriṉaiyuṭaiya vayaliṉiṭattu viḷainta nellai yaṟuppōr cilantiyiṉatu nūlkaḷ pakkattē cūḻnta pōrkaḷiṉ aṭiyai eṭuttu virippārkaḷ. piṉ ēr kaṭāviṭuvārkaḷ. vaikkōlaiyum kūḷattaiyum ataṉiṉiṉṟum nīkki mēṟkāṟṟilē tūvit tūṟṟiyaṉa poli malaipōlat tōṉṟum. ivvakaiyāṉa marutanilañ cērnta kuṭiyiruppukkaḷil vīṭṭiṉ pakkattē taṟikaḷil neṭiya kayiṟukaḷāl kaṉṟukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭu niṟkum. ēṇi eṭṭāta uyarattaiyuṭaiyatum talai tiṟantu uḷḷē coriyappaṭṭa paḻaiya nelliṉaiyuṭaiyatumākiya tāṉiyak kūṭukaḷ vīṭukaḷil uṇṭu. taccacciṟār ceyta ciṟu tērkaḷaip piḷḷaikaḷ uruṭṭic ceṉṟa kaḷaippālē cevilittāyaruṭaiya pālai niṟaiya uṇṭu paṭukkaiyilē tuyilvārkaḷ. ivvakaiyāṉa vaṟumai teriyāta kuṭiyiruppiṉaiyuṭaiya ūrilē nalla neṟcōṟṟiṉaik kōḻic cēvaliṉ camaitta poriyalōṭē peṟuvāy.
aṅkē pukai cūḻnta koṭṭilkaḷil karuppañcāṟṟaik kāyccuvārkaḷ. ak koṭṭilkaḷai aṭaintāl muṉ karuppañ cāṟṟaik koṭuttup piṉkaṭṭiyai uṇṇumpaṭi taruvārkaḷ.
appāṟ ceṉṟāl, valaiñar kuṭiyiruppai aṭaivīrkaḷ. avarkaḷ vīṭukaḷ kāñci vañci mutaliya maraṅkaḷiṉ kompukaḷaik kaikaḷukku naṭuvē tūṇāka naṭṭu, mūṅkil taṭikaḷai variccāka niṟaittut tāḻai nārāṟ kaṭṭit taruppaip pullāl vēynta tāḻnta iṟappai yuṭaiyaṉavākum. mīṉkaḷai vāri eṭukkum valaikaḷ muṟṟattilē kāṇappaṭum. talaivāyiliṉ kūraiyiṟ paṭarntuḷḷa curaikkoṭiyil kāykaḷ toṅkum. iḷaiyavarkaḷum mutiyavarkaḷum talaivāyiliṭattil kūṭiyiruntu, piṉ iṟālum kayalum piṟaḻum āḻnta kuḷaṅkaḷil piḷḷaikaḷōṭē ulāvi mīṉkaḷaip piṭippārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kuṭiyirup pil taṅkiṉāl, kuṟṟāta koḻiyalariciyaik kaḷiyākat tuḻāviyaṭṭa kūḻai, akaṉṟa vāyaiyuṭaiya taṭṭuppiḻāvilē ularavāṟṟip pāmpu kiṭakkiṉṟa puṟṟām paḻañcōṟṟiṉaiyokkum puṟattiṉaiyuṭaiya muḷaiyiṉai yiṭittuc cēra atilē kalantu cāṭiyiṉkaṇṇē muṟṟiya tum, viralālē alaittu arikkun taṉmaiyaiyuṭaiyatumākiya vevviya kaḷḷai mīṉcūṭṭuṭaṉ peṟuvāy. nīrttuṟaiyilē viḷaiyāṭi makaḷir pōkaṭṭuppōṉa kuḻaiyiṉai nīlaniṟamuḷḷa ciccilippaṟavai taṉakku iraiyeṉa eṭuttukkoṇṭu paṭcikaḷ niṟaintirukkum paṉaiyiṟ pōkātu antaṇar yākacālaiyil naṭṭa yūpattiṉmēlirukkum. atu cōṉakar pāymarattiṉ mēlēṟṟiya aṉṉaviḷakkaip pōlavum viṭiveḷḷi pōlavum oḷi viṭṭut tōṉṟum.
kaṭaṟkarai, mēṟṟicaikkaṇṇuḷḷa kutiraikaḷaiyum vaṭaticaik kaṇṇuḷḷa poruḷkaḷaiyum koṇṭu vantu tarum marakkalaṅkaḷ cūḻappeṟṟatu. maṇal mikunta terukkaḷil toḻilāḷarāṟ kākkappaṭum paṇṭakacālaikaḷum paratavar vāḻum vāṉaḷāviya māḷikaikaḷum uṇṭu. vīṭukaḷil viḷaiporuḷkaḷ tēṅkik kiṭakkum. uḻukiṉṟa erutu kaḷuṭaṉ pacukkaḷ neruṅkātapaṭi āṭṭukkiṭāykaḷum nāykaḷum cuḻaṉṟu tiriyum. uyarnta māṭattuṟaiyum pēraṇikalaṉkaḷaiyuṭaiya makaḷir paciya maṇikaḷ kōtta vaṭaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya alkuliṉiṭattē kiṭakkiṉṟa melliya tukilacaiya, malaiyilē āravārikkiṉṟa tōkaimayil pōl ulāvuvārkaḷ; kāliṭattēyuḷḷa poṟcilampukaḷ olikka, nūliṉāl varintu ceyyappaṭṭa pantiṉai aṭittu viḷaiyāṭuvārkaḷ. muttai yotta vārnta maṇalil metteṉak kaiyiṟṟaritta vaḷaikaḷ acaiyum paṭiyākap poṟkaḻaṅku koṇṭu viḷaiyāṭuvārkaḷ.
kaḷḷuṇpār palarum pukukiṉṟa vāyilil paciya koṭikaḷ acaiyum paṭi kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muṟṟattilē teyvattukkut tūviṉa civanta pūvāṭalkaḷ kiṭakkum. aṅkē kaḷḷaic camaikkiṉṟa makaḷir vaṭṭil kaḻuviya nīr vaṭitaliṉ nilañ cēṟāyirukkum. accēṟṟil puraḷukiṉṟa peṇ paṉṟiyuṭaṉē puṇarccik karuttāla pōkāmal kuḻiyilē niṟutti, nellai iṭitta māvākiya uṇaviṉāl āṇ paṉṟi vaḷarkkappaṭukiṟatu. ivvakaiyāṉa paṭṭiṉattil taṅkuvāyāyiṉ appaṉṟiyiṉ tacaiyōṭu kaḷḷai uṇṇumpaṭi peṟuvāy. tuṟaimukattiṉ kaṇṇē ākāyattai muṭṭumpaṭi uyarntatum kaṟṟai mutaliyavaṟṟāl vēyātatumākiya kalaṅ karai viḷakkam uṇṭu. itu marakkalaṅkaḷ tām cērum iṭattai yaṟintu cellumpaṭi niṟuvappaṭṭatu. itaṉaik kaṭantu ceṉṟāl teṉṉaṅkīṟṟu kaḷāl vēynta vīṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭum. ivvakaiyāṉa vīṭukaḷilē taṅkiṉāl palāppaḻattaiyum, iḷanīraiyum vāḻaippaḻattaiyum paṉai yiṉatu nuṅkōṭē vēṟu paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum muṟṟiṉa vaḷḷi mutaliya kiḻaṅkukaḷaiyum uṇṇumpaṭi peṟuvāy.
appāl ceṉṟāl kāntaḷ vaḷarukiṉṟa malaiyil yāṉai paṭin tāṟpōla, tiruveḵkāvil pāmpilē tuyil koḷḷum tirumālaik kāṇpāy. tiruveḵkāvaik kaṭantu ceṉṟāl kāñci nakarai aṭaivāy. aṅkē cōlaikaḷuḷ parikkōlaik kaiyilē uṭaiya yāṉaip pākar cōrntirukkumpōtu yāṉaikaḷukku neyyuṭaṉ kalantu vaikkappaṭṭa ariciyaic cūluṭaiya manti tiruṭik koṇṭu ceṉṟu uṇṇum. tērōṭutaliṉāl terukkaḷ tāḻntirukkiṉṟaṉa.
kaṭavuḷ uṟaivatum cītēvi vāḻvatumākiya avvaracaṉiṉ viyaṉakaraiyaṭaintu, avaṉaip pala naṉmoḻikaḷāṟ pukaḻntu yāḻ vācippiṉ, avaṉ ataṉaik kēṭṭu makiḻntu uṉṉuṭaiya araiyiṟ kiṭanta koṭṭaip pāciyiṉ vēraiyotta kiḻinta cīlaiyaip pōkkip, pālāviyai yotta, viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nūlāṟ ceyta tukilkaḷai uṉ kariya periya cuṟṟattārōṭē uṭukkap paṇṇi, vaḷainta arivāḷaik koṇṭa kaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya maṭaiyaṉ ākkiṉa iṟaicciyiṟ, koḻuviya tacaikaḷaiyum, cennel ariciyālākkiya cōṟṟaiyum, iṉiya cuvai yuṭaiya kaṇṭa carukkarai mutaliyavaṟṟuṭaṉē ūṭṭic ciṟiyavum periyavu mākiya veḷḷik kalaṅkaḷai uṅkaḷ piḷḷaikaḷukkiṭaiyē parappi, tāṉ ācaiyuṭaṉē avarkaḷai uṇṇappaṇṇi, poṟṟāmaraiyai nīṇṭa mayiriṭattē aḻakupeṟac coruki, nāṉku kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭiya tēraiyum paciya poṉṉāṟ ceyta cēṇattaiyum paricilākat taruvāṉ.

4. oḻipiyal
1. nālvakai eḻuttu
“ cīṉa pāṣaiyil ovvor eḻuttum ovvor karuttaik kuṟippa tāy iṉṟaḷavum pallāyiram eḻuttukkaḷai uṭaiya pāṣaiyāyiruk kiṟatu. tamiḻmoḻi eḻuttukkaḷiṉ āti caritamum, avvāṟē. avvā ṟeṉak kāṭṭu muṟaikaḷ pala tamiḻp pūrva ilakkaṇaṅkaḷuṭ kāṇalām. yāpparuṅkala viruttiyil ‘tērpata mutaliya nālvakai yeḻuttu, ukkira veḻuttu, muttiṟaveḻuttu, katiyeḻuttu, yōṉiyeḻuttu, caṅkēta veḻuttu mutaliyaṉavāyp palavita eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
tivākaram piṅkalantai mutaliya nūlkaḷil:-
‘peyareḻuttu muṭiveḻuttu vaṭiveḻuttut taṉmai eḻutteṉa veḻuttiṉ peyariyam piṉarē’ (tivākaram)
“vaṭivu peyar taṇmaiyuṇ muṭivu nāṉkā
naṭaipeṟu nāvalar nāṭiya veḻuttē” (ilak. kot)
“uruvē yuṇarvē yoliyē taṉmai
eṉavī reḻuttu mīriru pakutiya....” (ilak. kot)
ivaṟṟuḷ ‘vaṭiveḻutteṉṟatu cittirakkuṟikaḷaiyām. maṉitaṉai maṉitaṉiṉ uruvattaiyēṉum avaṉiṉ pākamākiya talaiyaiyēṉum cittirittukkāṭṭal peyareḻutteṉṟatu caṅkētakkuṟi. pōraik kuṟikka maṉitaṉiṉ puyaṅkaḷaik kāṭṭuvatu. taṉmai yeḻutteṉṟatu kuṇam, catti mutaliyavaṟṟaik kāṭṭa varaiyum aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ vivēkattukkuk kaṇ, veṟṟikkuk karuṭaṉ mutaliyaṉa varaital. muṭiveḻutteṉṟatu viṉai nikaḻcciyai uṇarttum vākkiyaṅkaḷaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiya vākkiyaṅkaḷ’ eṉṟār centamiḻp pattirāciriyarākiya tiru.mu.irākava aiyaṅkāravarkaḷ. tamiḻil ārāycci mikkuṭaiyarāyt ‘tirāviṭa vivakārattil teḷinta cila viṣayaṅkaḷ’ eṉṉum viyācameḻutiya ep je. riccarṭs paṇṭitar, ‘ātittamiḻ eḻuttu cittira vuruvamākavē uṇṭāyirukka vēṇṭum’ eṉak kūṟi, ataṟkuc cārpākap pallāriyil mayil malaiyil poṟit tirukkum cila cittiraṅkaḷaiyum paṭam piṭittu accēṟṟi veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. (tamiḻ ilakkiyam pa. 18-19)
2. maṭalēṟal
“maṭalēṟalāvatu, oruvaṉ ovvāk kāmattāl paṉaṅkarukkāṟ kutiraiyum, paṉantaruvi ṉuḷḷaṉavaṟṟāl vaṇṭil mutalāṉavuñ ceytu ak kutiraiyiṉ mēlēṟuvatu. maṭalēṟuvāṉ tikamparaṉāy uṭaleṅkum nīṟupūcik kiḻi ōviyar kaippaṭātu tāṉē tīṭṭik kiḻiyiṉ talaippuṟattil avaṉ peyarai varaintu kaippiṭittu, ūr naṭuvē nāṟcantiyil ākāra nittiraiyiṉṟi, akkiḻimēṟ pārvaiyuñ cintaiyu mirutti, vēṭkai vayattaṉāy vēṟuṇarviṉṟi, āvūriṉum, aḻal mēṟpaṭiṉum aṟita liṉṟi, maḻai veyil kāṟṟāṉ mayaṅkātiruppuḻi, avvūriṉuḷḷār palaruṅ kūṭivantu nī maṭalēṟutiyō? avaḷait tarutum, cōtaṉai tarutiyō? eṉṟavaḻi, iyaintāṉāyiṉ, aracaṉukkaṟivittu, avaṉē valāl avaṉ iṉaintu naiyattantu maṭalēṟeṉṟavaḻi, ēṟu muṟaimai - pūḷai yelumpu erukku ivaikaḷāṟ kaṭṭiya mālaiyaṇintukoṇṭu ammāvilēṟa, avvaṭattai vītiyilīrttalum, avvuruḷai yuruṇ ṭōṭumpoḻutu, paṉaṅkarukku aṟutta iṭamellām irattan tōṉṟātu vīriyantōṉṟiṉ, appoḻutu avaḷai alaṅkarittuk koṭuppatu, irattaṅ kaṇṭuḻi, avaṉaik kolai ceytuviṭuvatu. ivai pulavarāl nāṭṭiya vaḻakkeṉṟuṇarka.” (tañ. kōvai-urai)
“māveṉa maṭalu mūrpa pūveṉak
kuvimuki ḻerukkaṅ kaṇṇiyuñ cūṭupa
maṟuki ṉārkkavum paṭupa
piṟitu mākupa kāmaṅkāḻk koḷiṉē” (kuṟun-17)
“viḻuttalaip peṇṇai viḷaiyaṉ māmaṭaṉ
maṇiyaṇi peruntār mārpiṟ pūṭṭi
veḷḷeṉ paṇintupiṟa reḷḷat tōṉṟi
yorunāṇ maruṅkiṟ perunā ṇīkkit
teruvi ṉiyalavun taruvatu kollō
kaliṅkavi racainaṭaip pētai
melintila ṇāmviṭaṟ kamainta tūtē” (kuṟun-182)
“ciṟumaṇi toṭarntu peruṅkaccu niṟīik
kuṟumuki ḻerukkaṅ kaṇṇi cūṭi
yuṇṇā naṉmāp paṇṇi yemmuṭaṉ
maṟukuṭaṉ tiritaruñ ciṟukuṟu mākkaḷ” (naṟṟiṉai-220)
kirāma paripālaṉam
“ceṅkaṟpaṭṭuc cillāvil, uttaramallūril, vaikuṇṭa perumāḷ ālayattil ki.pi. pattāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil parakēcarivarmaṉ eṉṟa cōḻaṉ ēṟpaṭuttiya oru cilācācaṉattil, uttara mallūraic cērnta kirāma paripālaṉa vitikaḷaic cīrtiruttiya viparam kaṇṭirukkiṟatu. atil capaiyārai niyamittalāṉa oḻuṅku viḷakkiyiruk kiṟatu. periyarum ciṟiyarumāṉa kirāmavācikaḷ ōriṭattil kūṭṭaṅ kūṭa vēṇṭum. ālayaṅkaḷiṉ arccakarkaḷ yāvarum kūṭṭattil vantirukka vēṇṭum. capaiyāka niyamikkattakkavarkaḷ peyarkaḷai ovvoru ōlainaṟukkil eḻuti antanta nattam vāriyākat taṉittaṉik kaṭṭukaḷākak kaṭṭi vaikka vēṇṭum. arccakarkaḷil vayatu mutirntōr veṟuṅ kuṭamoṉṟaik kaiyileṭuttuk koṇṭu kūṭṭattiṉ mattiyil niṟka vēṇṭum. ōlaik kaṭṭukaḷai ovvoṉṟākak kuṭattaik kulukki oṉṟu maṟiyāta oru ciṟuvaṉai aḻaittuk kuṭattil kaiyiṭṭu oru naṟukkai eṭukkac ceytu, atai mattiyattar kaiyiṟ koṭukka vēṇṭum. anta naṟukkiluḷḷa peyarai mattiyattar vācitta piṟaku maṟṟa arccakarkaḷ ovvoruvarum vācittuk kāṭṭa vēṇṭum. ataṉ mēl antantap peyar ellārukkum aṅkīrakāramākum. iṅṅaṉam nattattuk kov voruvarākak kirāma paripālaṉa makācapai ēṟpaṭuttuvateṉṟu mēṟpaṭi cācaṉattil kaṇṭirukkiṟatu.” (celvakēcavarāya mutaliyār)
1oru poṉ eṉappaṭṭa taṅka nāṇayam iraṇṭarai veḷḷi nāṇayam (rūpā) peṟumatiyuḷḷatu . -tamiḻārāycci (pa.260)
3. caṅka nūlkaḷ
pattuppāṭṭu eṭṭuttokai patiṉeṇkīḻk kaṇakku mutaliyaṉa caṅka nūlkaḷeṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. patiṉeṇkīḻk kaṇakkiluḷḷa nūlkaḷuṭ perumpālaṉa caṅkakālattukkup piṟpaṭṭaṉa.
“muruku porunāṟu pāṇiraṇṭu mullai
peruku vaḷamaturaik kāñci-maruviṉiya
kōlaneṭu nalvāṭai kōlkuṟiñci paṭṭiṉap
pālai kaṭāttoṭum pattu”
tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai, ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭai, perumpāṇāṟ ṟuppaṭai, mullaippāṭṭu, maturaikkāñci, neṭunalvāṭai, kuṟiñcip pāṭṭu, paṭṭiṉappālai, malaipaṭukaṭām eṉpaṉa pattuppāṭṭil aṭaṅkiya nūlkaḷām:
eṭṭuttokaiyil aṭaṅkiya nūlkaḷ:
“naṟṟiṇai nalla kuṟuntokai yaiṅkuṟunū
ṟotta patiṟṟuppat tōṅku paripāṭal
kaṟṟaṟintār pōṟṟuṅ kaliyō ṭakampuṟameṉ
ṟittiṟatta veṭṭut tokai.”
puṟanāṉūṟu, patiṟṟuppattu, aiṅkuṟunūṟu, kalittokai, paripāṭal, akanāṉūṟu, naṟṟiṇai, kuṟuntokai eṉpaṉavām.
1. patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷ
‘nālaṭi nāṉmaṇi nāṉāṟpa taintiṇaimup
pālkaṭukaṅ kōvai paḻamoḻi-māmūla
meynnilaiya kāñciyō ṭēlāti yeṉpavē
kainnilaiya vāṅkīḻk kaṇakku.”
nālaṭiyār, nāṉmaṇikkaṭikai, iṉiyavai nāṟpatu, iṉṉā nāṟpatu, kār nāṟpatu, kaḷavaḻi nāṟpatu, tirikaṭukam, ācārakkōvai, paḻamoḻi, ciṟu pañcamūlam, mutumoḻikkāñci, ēlāti, aintiṇai aimpatu, tiṇai moḻi aimpatu, aintiṇai eḻupatu, tiṇaimālai nūṟṟaimpatu, tirukkuṟaḷ eṉpaṉavām.
kaṭaiciyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa āṟunūlkaḷaip paṟṟip palvēṟu apipparāyaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
“kaṭaiccaṅka nūlkaḷil peyar vaḻaṅkum pulavar kiṭṭattaṭṭa ainnūṟṟuvar eṉak kūṟappaṭṭatu. ivaruḷ perumpālār iṉṉāreṉa aṟitaṟkiṭamillai. oruvāṟu iṉṉāreṉa aṟiyak kiṭantavar irunūṟu pulavarē. ivaruḷ vēḷāḷar aimpattoruvar, peṇpālār muppat taṟuvar, antaṇar irupattoṉpatiṉmar, nākar patiṉeḻuvar; eyiṉar patiṉmūvar, kammāḷar eḻuvar, vaṇikar eḻuvar, maṉṉar aivar; āyar mūvar; kuyavar oruvar, paratavar oruvar, vaḷḷuvar oruvar, pāṇṭiya maṉṉar patiṉmūvar, cēramaṉṉar eḻuvar, cōḻamaṉṉar oruvar, toṇṭaimāṉ oruvar. (tamiḻ.ilak.pa.66)
2. aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷ
cilappatikāram, cintāmaṇi, maṇimēkalai, vaḷaiyāpati, kuṇṭalakēci.
3. aiñciṟu kāppiyaṅkaḷ
cūḷāmaṇi, utayaṇaṉ katai, nīlakēci, yacōtara kāviyam. nākakumāra kāviyam.
4. tirukkuṟaḷ
tirukkuṟaḷ ukkirapperuvaḻuti kālattil tamiḻccaṅkattil araṅkēṟṟappaṭṭateṉpatu kaṉṉa paramparaik katai. ilaṅkaiyai yāṇṭa ēllāḷaṉ (ēlēlaciṅkaṉ) kālattavar ivar eṉac cilar kūṟuvar. appaṭiyāyiṉ avar kālam ki.mu.204 ākum. tiruvaḷḷuvar ceytuḷḷa nūl ellāc camayattārālum eccātiyārālum koṇṭāṭappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. tiruvaḷḷuvar tamatu nūliṟ kūṟiyuḷḷa camayamē tamiḻarkaḷ camayam eṉṟu palar apippapirāyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. tiruvaḷḷuvar maṉu nūlilō cāṇakkiyar ceyta arttacāttirattilō viruntu tamatu nūlukku vēṇṭiya ātāraṅkaḷaip peṟṟār eṉṟu oru cilarum, aṟṟaṉṟu, mēṟpaṭi nūlkaḷiluḷḷa palavaṟṟaiyē moḻi peyart tuḷḷār eṉṟu vēṟu cilaruṅkūṟuvar. cantirakuptaṉukku mantiriyāyirunta cāṇakkiyar (kauṭaliyar) aruttacāttiram, kāmacāttiram, taruma cāttiram. mōṭca cāttiram mutaliya pala nūlkaḷai vaṭa moḻiyil cey tuḷḷār eṉṟum, avar tamiḻnāṭṭiṉar eṉṟum aṟikiṉṟōm. cāṇakkiyar tamiḻnāṭṭil aṟiyappaṭṭatum vaṭanāṭṭil aṟiyappaṭāta tumākiya poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟiya nūlkaḷai iyaṟṟiṉamaiyāl an nūlkaḷ vaṭanāṭṭil mikavum pukaḻ peṟṟu viḷaṅka ētuvāyiṉa. cāṇak kiyar ceytuḷḷa nūlkaḷukku ātāram tamiḻakattil nūl vaḻakkilō cevi vaḻakkilō uḷḷa poruḷkaḷai ātāramākak koṇṭu nūl iyaṟṟiya cāṇakkiyar tiruvaḷḷuvar eṉṉum iruvar nūlkaḷilum ōrōriṭaṅkaḷil orumaippāṭu kāṇappaṭutal iyalpē. ākavē, vaḷḷuvar cāṇakkiyar nūliliruntu cilavaṟṟai eṭuttu moḻipeyarttuk koṇṭār eṉak kūṟutal iyaipuṭaiyatākātu. maṉu nūlukkum tirukkuṟaḷukkum uḷḷa vittiyācam aṇuvukkum māmēruvukkum uḷḷa tākum. itaṉ vēṟṟumaiyai āciriyar cuntaram piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ, “vaḷḷuvar cey tirukkuṟaḷai vaḻuvaṟa naṉ kuṇarntōhkaḷ - uḷḷuvarō maṉuvāti orukulattuk korunīti” eṉṟu naṉi viḷakkiyuḷḷār. viḷai yāṭṭuc ciṟārāl maṇaliṟ kīṟappaṭṭa kōṭukaḷilum, nattai ōṭṭiluḷḷa kīṟukaḷilum ōrōviṭattu akara vaṭivam kāṇappaṭuvatu pōla maṉunūli luḷḷa ētō oru vākkiyam tirukkuṟaḷiluḷḷa yātāṉumoru poru ḷōṭu oṟṟumaippaṭumāyiṉ, muṉṉatiṉ moḻipeyarppē piṉṉatu eṉṟu vātāṭutal apimāṉam paṟṟiyatākum.
5. tiruvaḷḷuvamālai
tiruvaḷḷuvaraiyum avar nūlaiyum ciṟappittup pāṭappaṭṭa veṇpākkaḷ tiruvaḷḷuvamālai eṉa vaḻaṅkum. ippāṭalkaḷ ki.pi.eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil yārō oru pulavarāl pāṭap paṭṭateṉac cila aṟiñar apippirāyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
“tiruvaḷḷuvamālai ceytatu kuṟaḷ araṅkēṟṟiya kālatteṉṟā vatu, allatu nāmakaḷ iṟaiyaṉār mutaliyavarkaḷ pāṭiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭum ceyyuḷkaḷ, meymmaiyāka avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟāvatu koḷḷaṟkiṭamiṉmaiyāl,mēṟkūṟiya māmūlaṉār pāṭ ṭeṉpatu vaḷḷuvarai iḻikulattāṉeṉpataṟkut takka ātāramākātu. tiruvaḷḷuvamālai ceytatu kuṟaḷ araṅkēṟṟiya kālattaṉṟeṉpataṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa eṉpīrāyiṉ, acarīriyum, nāmakaḷum pāṭṭup pāṭuvatu aṉupava virōtamum acampāvitamumēyām. maṟṟaiyōrkaḷāṟ pāṭap paṭṭaṉavākak kāṇappaṭum pāṭṭukkaḷuṭ perumpālaṉa meyyāka avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉavalla. eṅṅaṉameṉiṉ, oru nūlukkup pāyirañ collavō aṉṟi ataṉai eṭuttup pukaḻavō pāyirañ collavō aṉṟi ataṉaip eṭuttup pukaḻavō pukkār annūṉ muḻuvataiyum paṟṟip pēcuvarēyaṉṟi, ataṉ oru pākattai māttiram pēcār. tiruvaḷḷuvamālaiyilō aṟattup pālaippaṟṟi,
—“pāyira nāṉkil laṟamirupāṉ paṉmūṉṟē
tūya tuṟavaṟa moṉ ṟūḻāka-āya
aṟattuppāl nālvakaiyā yāynturaittār nūliṉ
tiṟattuppāl vaḷḷuvaṉār tērntu.”
eṉṟu eṟiccalūr malāṭaṉārum, poruṭpālaippaṟṟi,
“araciya laiyain tamaicciya līrain
turuval laraṇiraṇṭoṉ ṟoṇkūḻ- iruviyal
tiṇpaṭai naṭpup patiṉēḻ kuṭipatiṉmūṉ
ṟeṉporu ḷēḻā mivai”
eṉṟu pōkkiyārum, kāmattup pālaippaṟṟi,
“āṇpālē ḻāṟiraṇṭu peṇpā laṭuttaṉpu
pūṇpā lirupālō rāṟāka-māṇpāya
kāmattiṉ pakkamoru mūṉṟākak kaṭṭuraittār
nāmattiṉ vaḷḷuvaṉār naṉku”
eṉṟu mōcikīraṉārum kūṟiṉatāka varukiṉṟatu. immūṉṟu pāṭalum kuṟaḷiluḷḷa ovvōriyaliṉ tokaiyaiyum atikārattiṉ tokaiyai yum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa vaṉṟik kuṟaḷiṉ ciṟappai allatu ataṉ arumai perumaikaḷai eṭutturaikkavillai. aṉṟic ceyyuḷaḻaku vāyntaṉa vāyumillai.ivaikaḷai iyaṟṟuvataṟku mūṉṟu caṅkap pulavarkaḷ vēṇṭiyatillai; kārikai kaṟṟuk kavipāṭum oruvar pōtum. iṅṅaṉamē ivai mūṉṟum kuṟaḷiṉ iyalum atikāramum ittaṉaiyeṉṟu kaṇak kiṭa virumpiya oruvarālēyē iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum.
tiruvaḷḷuvamālai kuṟaḷ araṅkēṟṟappaṭṭa kālattuc ceyyap paṭavillai eṉpataṟku maṟṟoru niyāyamum kūṟutum. am mālaiyil ukkirapperuvaḻutiyār pāṭiṉatāka varum ceyyuḷil ‘nāṉ maṟaiyiṉ meypporuḷai mupporuḷā nāṉmukattōṉ tāṉ maṟaintu vaḷḷuvaṉāyt tanturaitta nūl’ eṉṟum, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattuk kārikkaṇṇaṉār peyar koṇṭa ceyyuḷil, ‘aiyāṟu nūṟu matikāra mūṉṟumā, meyyāya vētap poruḷ viḷaṅkap-poyyātu, tantā ṉulakukkut tāṉvaḷḷuvaṉāki, antā maraimē layaṉ’ eṉṟum vaḷḷuvaraip piramatēvaṉiṉ avatāramākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivvāṟu vaḷḷuvaraip piramatēvaṉiṉ avatāramākak kūṟavantatu avar uyirō ṭiruntapōtaṉṟu. uyirōṭirukkumpōtē oruvaraik kaṭavuḷiṉ avatāramākak koṇṭāṭuvatu eṅkumillai. oruvar iṟantapiṉ pala varuṭaṅkaḷ ceṉṟu kālam nīṭikka nīṭikkattāṉ avaruṭaiya perumai vaḷarcciyuṟṟut teyvat taṉmaiyaṭaiyum. appōtutāṉ avar kaṭavuḷiṉ avatāramākak karutappaṭuvār. aṅṅaṉamē vaḷḷuvariṉ aṟiviṉ perumaiyaik kaṇṭu avarai oru kaṭavuḷiṉ avatāramākak koṇṭāṭa vantatu avar kuṟaḷiyaṟṟip pala varuṭaṅkaḷ ceṉṟapiṉpē yām. appaṭic ciṟitu kālañ ceṉṟu vaḷḷuvar piramatēvaṉuṭaiya avatārameṉṟa koḷkai vērūṉṟiyataṉ piṉṉarē intap pāṭṭukkaḷ pāṭappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ippāṭalkaḷ pāṭappaṭṭatu kuṟaḷ iyaṟṟiya veku kālattukkup piṉpāṉāl, avaṟṟai aṭakkiyuḷḷa tiruvaḷḷuvamālai kuṟaḷaraṅkēṟṟappaṭṭa kālattu iyaṟṟappaṭṭa tākātu.
iṉi iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyaluraiyil kaṭaic caṅkap pulavar eṇmar peyarkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivveṇmaruḷ tiruvaḷḷuvamālai yil mūvar peyar māttiram kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; aivar peyarillai. aṉṟiyum cilappatikārattila makā makōpāttiyāyar cāmināta aiyaravar kaḷāl eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa paḻaiya akavaloṉṟu kaṭaiccaṅkap pulavaril irupatteḻuvar peyar kūṟukiṉṟatu. avaṟṟil patiṉēḻu peyar tiruvaḷḷuvamālaiyil illai. itaṉāl tiruvaḷḷuvamālai pāṭiṉavar kaṭaiccaṅkap pulavarkaḷ eṉṟu colvataṟkiṭamiṉṟākiṉṟatu.
mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟāl tiruvaḷḷuvamālai kaṭaiccaṅkakālattu nūlaṉṟeṉpatum, atiluḷḷa pāṭṭoṉṟēṉum aṅkē kūṟappaṭṭa pulavarālēyē iyaṟṟappaṭṭateṉpataṟkut takka ātāram illai eṉpatum naṉku pōtarum. āyiṉ tiruvaḷḷuvamālai ekkālattu yārāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭateṉiṉ, kālantōṟum tirukkuṟaḷaip paṟṟip pāṭṭukkaḷ pala pulavarāliyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavāka, piṟkālattu vanta pulavaroruvar avaṟṟaiyellām, aṉṟēl, avaṟṟuṭ ciṟantavaṟṟai māttiram oruṅku cērttu oḻuṅkupaṭutti oru nūlākkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. avar tāmum cila pāṭṭukkaḷ pāṭic cērttirukkalām. allatu nūṉ muḻuvataiyum oru pulavarē ceytumirukkalām. vaḷḷuvamālaiyil varum pāṭṭukkaḷ ēṟakkuṟaiya orē vitamāṉa nēricai veṇpākkaḷāka viruntālum, acarīri coṉṉatāka varum pāṭṭu acarīriyē nērē coṉṉa tāyillāmal,
“tiruttaku teyvat tiruvaḷ ḷuvarō
ṭuruttaku naṟpalakai yokka-irukka
uruttira caṉma reṉavuraittu vāṉil
orukkavō veṉṟatōr col”
eṉa, acarīri coṉṉatāka maṟṟoruvarāl pāṭappaṭṭiruttalum intak koḷkaikkut takunta tuṇaiyākum.
iṉṉum intap pāṭṭu maturaiyil teyvattaṉmai poruntiya caṅkappalakai oṉṟiruntateṉpataik kuṟittaliṉālum, antap palakaiyil tiruvaḷḷuvarō ṭokkaviruttaṟku uruttiracaṉmarē ēṟṟavareṉpata ṉāl, kuṟaḷaraṅkēṟṟiya kālattil caṅkappulavar nāṟpatteṇmarum tiruvaḷḷuvarō ṭokkavirukkat takutiyaṟṟavarāyp poṟṟāmarai vāviyuḷ vīḻntu mūḻkiṉareṉṉuṅ kataikkut tōṟṟumiṭamā yiruttali ṉālum, itu kuṟaḷ tōṉṟi veku kālattukkup piṉ iyaṟṟappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭumeṉpatu tēṟṟam. caṅkap palakaiyilēṟṟiya intak katai kaṭṭukkatai. itu pōṉṟatoru katai caṭakōpariyaṟṟiya tiruvāy moḻiyaippaṟṟiyum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.” (tirukkōṇamalai vittuva cirōmaṇi ta.kaṉakacuntaram piḷḷai. pi.ē.)
1tiruvaḷḷuvamālai eṉṉum nūl cantēkattukkuriya kataikaḷum kaṟpaṉaikaḷum niṟainta pāṭalkaḷ aṭaṅkiyatu. ippāṭalkaḷ ki.pi. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil tiruvaḷḷuvar nūlaic ciṟappikka yārō oru tamiḻppulavarāl pāṭappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. - tamiḻārāycci pa.247
4. makaḷiraic ciṟaippiṭittal
muṉṉāḷil ōr aracaṉ piṟaṉōr aracaṉōṭu porutuveṉ ṟāl, avaṉ nāṭṭiluḷḷa makaḷiraic ciṟaippiṭittu vanta avaraik kaṟpaḻittum māṉapaṅkañ ceytum varuttāmal pātukāttuk kōyil kaḷil kaṭavuḷait toḻutukoṇṭu aṅkuk kaṭavuṭṭiruppaṇi ceyyumpaṭi iruttuvāṉ.
5. kaṭaṟpōr
cēralātaṉ kaṭaliṭattē tīvoṉṟil vacitta taṉ pakaivarmēṟ kappaṟpaṭaiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu, avaratu kāvaṉ maramāṉa kaṭampai veṭṭi yeṟintu, avaṉaip pōr tolaittāṉ. ivvaralāṟu patiṟṟuppattil kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
6. tamiḻaracar mālaikaḷ
pāṇṭiyaṉ-vēmpu, cēraṉ-paṉai, cōḻaṉ-ātti. “pōntai vēmpē āreṉa varūum māperuntāṉaiyar”(tol)
7. paṭaikaḷiṉ aṇivakuppu mutaliyaṉa
aṇi, uṇṭai, ōṭṭu, ākkam, koṭippaṭai, tār, tūci, niṟai, kūḻai.
8. akaḻikaḷ
cila akaḻikaḷ 40 aṭit tāḻvu iruntaṉa. ki.pi. 781 ilum 977 ilum eḻutappaṭṭa cila cilācācaṉaṅkaḷ itaik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. - (Studies in Tamil Literature and History p.23)
9. pōrmuracu
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kaṉakavicayaruṭaṉ yuttattukkup puṟappaṭṭapōtu koṭumpaṟai, neṭuvayir, muracam, pāṇṭil, mayirkkaṇ muracam mutaliyaṉa olikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu cilappatikārattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
10. ilavantikaippaḷḷi
aracaṉ araṇmaṉaiyil vācañceyvatu māttiramallāmal cōlaikaḷiṉ mattiyil amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kuḷircci poruntiya māḷikaiyilum kōṭaikkālaṅkaḷil vacittāṉ. ammāḷikai ilavantikaippaḷḷi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉkaṇ kāṟṟu vacatiyum nīr vacatiyum porunta amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa maṇṭapam ‘nīrāḻi maṇṭapam’ eṉappaṭṭatu.
11. aracaṉait terivu ceyyum nūtaṉa muṟai
ōr aracaṉiṉ maraṇattukkuppiṉ irācciyapāram ēṟpavar illātapoḻutum, irācciyattiṉ urimaikkup palar vātāṭum poḻutum paṭṭattuyāṉai vāyilāka aracaṉait terivu ceytalum muṉṉāḷ vaḻakku eṉpatu,
“kaḻumalat tiyātta kaḷiṟum karuvūr
viḻumiyōṉ mēṟceṉ ṟataṉāl-viḻumiya
vēṇṭiṉum vēṇṭā viṭiṉum uṟaṟpāla
tīṇṭā viṭutal aritu” (paḻamoḻi-26)
eṉṉum paḻamoḻi veṇpāvāl aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
12. araca taṇṭaṉaikaḷ
kaḷavu, viyapicāram, irācatturōkam mutaliyavaṟṟukkut takunta taṇṭaṉai ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭiruntatu. uṟuppukkaḷai veṭṭutal, ciṟaiyil aṭaittal, kolai ceytal eṉat taṇṭam mūṉṟu vakaippaṭṭatu. irācat turōkattukkuk kolaiyum, viyapicārattiṟkuk kāl kuṟaitta lum muṟaiyākaviruntaṉa.
“kāṇiṟ kuṭippaḻiyāṅ kaiyuṟiṟ kāl kuṟaiyum” (nālaṭi. 34)
“kolaiyiṟ koṭiyārai vēntoṟuttal paiṅkūḻ
kaḷaikaṭ ṭataṉoṭu nēr” (kuṟaḷ. 550)
13. kirāmac caṅkam
kirāma kāriyaṅkaḷai vayatiṉ mutiyavarkaḷ ūr naṭuviluḷḷa maraṅkaḷiṉ kīḻk kūṭi muṭivu ceytārkaḷ. aṅṅaṉam kūṭum caṅkam maṉṟam allatu potiyil eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭṭatu. immaṉṟaṅkaḷ perum pālum, āl, viḷā, palā, vēmpu mutaliya maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻk kūṭiṉa. marattaic cūḻntu nāṟpuṟamum tiṇṇai amaikkappaṭṭiruntatu.
14. tuṟavikaḷ tōṟṟam
maravuriyai uṭaiyākac ceyta uṭaiyiṉar. aḻakālum vaṭivālum niṟattālum valampuric caṅkaiyokkum narai muṭiyiṉar. ekkālattum nīrāṭutaliṉ aḻukkillāmal viḷaṅkum mēṉiyar. aṭikkaṭi viratamiruntu paṭṭiṉi kiṭappatāl tacaiyillāta mārpiṉ elumpukaḷiṉ kōvai veḷippaṭat tōṉṟi ulavum uṭampiṉaiyuṭaiyavar. epporuḷum nukarvataṟku naṉṟākiya pakaṟpoḻutukaḷ pala cērakkaḻinta vuṇaviṉai yuṭaiyār. neṭuṅkālam maṉattil taṅkakkūṭiya kōpattaip pōkkiya maṉattiṉar. palavaṟṟaiyum kaṟṟa perum kalvimāṉkaḷum ciṟitum aṟiya māṭṭāta iyalpāṉa aṟivuṭaiyavar. palavaṟṟaiyum kaṟṟōrkkut tām ellaiyākiya talaimaiyaiyuṭaiyavar. ācaiyōṭē kūṭiya ciṉattai yum pōkkiṉa aṟiviṉaiyuṭaiyavar. tavattāl uṭalukku varuttam iruppatu uṇmaiyeṉiṉum taṅkaḷ maṉattiṉāl varuttam ciṟitu maṟiyappaṭāta iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiyavar. (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai)
15. makaḷir aḻaku
oru pāṭiṉiyiṉ aḻakiṉ viparam porunarāṟṟuppaṭaiyil kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. atu varumāṟu: kūntal vārnta āṟṟiṉ karumaṇal pōṉṟatu. neṟṟi piṟai pōṉṟatu. puruvam viṟpōṉṟatu, kuḷircci poruntiya kaṇ aḻakiya kaṭaiyaiyuṭaiyatu, vāy ilavitaḻ pōṉṟatu, paṟkaḷ veḷḷiya muttukkaḷ pōṉṟaṉa, kātukaḷ kattirikai pōṉṟaṉa. avai makarakkuḻai aṇiyappeṟṟu acaiyun taṉmaiyuṭaiyaṉa, tōḷkaḷ mūṅkilpōṟ perumaiyuṭaiyaṉa, muṉkaikaḷ aitākiya mayiriṉaiyuṭaiyaṉa, viralkaḷ kāntaḷmalar pōṉṟaṉa, nakaṅkaḷ kiḷiyiṉatu vāy pōṉṟaṉa, mulaikaḷ īrkkiṭai pōkātu paṇaittaṉa, koppūḻ nīrccuḻi pōṉṟatu, iṭai ciṟumaiyaiyuṭaiyatu. alkul palamaṇi kōtta mēkalai aṇiyappaṭṭatu, kuṟaṅku piṭiyiṉatu kai pōṉṟatu. aṭikaḷ ōṭi iḷaitta nāyiṉatu nākkup pōṉṟaṉa, koṇṭai vāḻaippūp pōṉṟatu.
16. tamiḻar vāṇipam
1paḻaiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷum kirēkka urōmaṉ nūlāciriyar kaḷum kiṟittu muṉṉiraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷilum cōḻa irācciyattaic cērnta kiḻakkukkarai kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷōṭum mēṟkut tēcaṅkaḷōṭum periya vāṇipam naṭattuvataṟkiṭaṉāyiruntatu. cōḻa tēcak kaṭa lōṭikaḷ karaiyōraṅkaḷil māttiram pirayāṇañceytārallar. avarkaḷ vaṅkāḷak kuṭākkaṭalaik kaṭantu kaṅkānati airāvati mukattu vāraṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ; intu camuttirattaik kaṭantu malāyt tīvuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷai aṭaintārkaḷ. (viṉceṉṭ cimit)
17. nakar kāvalar
ivar pulipōlunar. tuyirkoḷḷāta kaṇṇar. añcāneñcar. kaḷavutoḻil tantiraṅkaḷaik kaṇṭaṟiyum nuṇṇiya aṟiviṉar. kuṟitappā ampiṉar. terukkaḷil maḻainīr perukkeṭuttōṭum kāriruḷ kavinta yāmaṅkaḷilum ivar iḷaippiṉṟi ūr cuṟṟi varuvār. kaṟutta uṭaliṉa rāyk karuñcēlai yuṭuttu vāḷ kaikkoṇṭu meṉṉūlēṇi araiyiṟ cuṟṟi viḻitta kaṇ imaikku muṉ maṟaintōṭum valiyuṭaiya kaḷvaraiyum kaṭitiṟ piṭittukkoṇarum āṟṟaluṭaiyavar. (maturaik kāñci)
18. iṉṉa pulavar iṉṉa pāvil tiṟamaiyuṭaiyāreṉpatu.
“veṇpāviṟ pukaḻēnti paraṇikkōr
cayaṅkoṇṭāṉ virutta meṉṉum
oṇpāvi luyarkampaṉ kōvaiyulā
antātik koṭṭak kūttaṉ
kaṇpāya kalampakattiṟ kiraṭṭaiyarkaḷ
vacaipāṭak kāḷa mēkam
paṇpāya pakarcantam paṭikkāca
lātoruvar pakaro ṇātē.”
19. tamiḻ ārāycci1 nūliṟ kāṇum cila kuṟippukkaḷ
2āriyar varukaikku muṉṉamēyē eḻuttuc cuvaṭi mutaliya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ uṇmaiyāl akattiyar vaṭakkiliruntu eḻuttaik koṇṭu vantār eṉpatu uṇmaiyaṉṟu. -tamiḻārāycci pakkam 122.
payirttoḻil, aṅkanūl, ciṟpakkalai, kaṭṭaṭak kalai, vāṉanūl, vāṇikam, vīṭṭupporuḷkaḷ, uṟaviṉ muṟaikaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ. paṟavaikaḷ. tāvaraṅkaḷ, moḻi, ilakkiyam maruntu, ulōkaṅkaḷ, araciyal, camayam, yuttam, niṟai , aḷavu mutaliyavaikaḷum piṟavumākiya vaṟṟaip pulappaṭuttum paḻaiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷuṇṭu.
20. ēṟu taḻuvutal
ēṟu taḻuvutal āyarukkē uriyatāyiruntatu. piṟai pōṉṟa kōṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiya ēṟṟiṉ valiyai aṭakkiya ciṟuvarukku āyar tam makaḷirai vatuvai cūṭṭiṉar. ēṟu taḻuvuṅkāl avvēṟukaḷ taṅ kūrṅkōṭukaḷāl ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ vayiṟṟaip piḷantu avaraik kolvatu muṇṭu. itaṉ viparam mullaik kaliyil naṉku kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
“avviṭattu ēṟukaḷait taḻuvippōkkumpaṭiyai uṭkoṇṭu vantu tiraṇṭu maḻai muḻakkeṉṉa iṭiyeṉṉa naṭuvāṉilattiṉ muṉṉē āravārameḻap pukaiyōṭu pukaiyeḻa ēṟu taḻuviṉārkkuk koṭuttaṟku nalla makaḷir tiraṇṭu niṟpa, nīrt tuṟaiyilum ālamarattiṉ kīḻum valiyiṉaiyuṭaiya marāmarattiṉ kīḻum uṟaiyun teyvaṅkaṭkuc ceyyum muṟaimaikaḷaip paravit toḻuvilē pāyntār.
“erutiṉatu nōkkai añcāṉāy ataṉmēl pāynta iṭai yaṉaic cāvak kuttik kompiṭattē eṭuttukkoṇṭu uṭalaik kulaik kiṉṟa tōṟṟaravaik kāṇāy; aḻakiya talaimayiriṉaiyuṭaiya maṉamacainta iyalpiṉai yuṭaiyaḷākiya turōpataiyatu tuyya kūntalilē kaiyai nīṭṭiya tuccātaṉaṉuṭaiya neñcaippiḷantu pōkaṭṭup pakaivar naṭuvē tāṉ coṉṉa vañciṉattai vāykkac ceyta vīmacēṉaṉaip pōlum” (kali. 101)
“ēṟākiya erumaiyai yēṟukiṉṟa kūṟṟuvaṉuṭaiya neñcai vaṭimpālē piḷantu pōkaṭṭuc ciṉattōṭē ariya uyirai vāṅkiṉa aññāṉṟai yiṟaivaṉ ittaṉmaiyai uṭaiyavaṉko leṉṟu kūṟumpaṭiyākak kāṟṟiṉ vicaipōla ōṭi vantu viraintu ēṟākiya kāriyataṉaip palarum vantu cērtalaiyuṭaiya kaḷattē valiyaṭaṅkat taḻuvi varutti ataṉmēlē tōṉṟi niṉṟa potuvaṉatu aḻakaip pārāy; ataṉaik kaṇṭu eṉ neñcu uṭkiṟṟukkā ṇeṉṟāḷ.” (kali. 103)
21. paḻaiya maṇamuṟai
cōṟu neyyuṭaṉ mutiyōrukku aḷikkappaṭṭatu. paṭcikaḷ naṉṉimittaṅ kāṭṭiṉa. periya vāṉam kaḷaṅkamiṉṟi yiruntatu. cantiraṉ kuṟṟamillātu rōkiṇiyuṭaṉ poruntiyiruntāṉ. maṇappantal alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu. teyvattai vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ. periya muḻavu olittatu. nīrāṭṭi alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa maṇappoṇṇai ēṉaiya peṇkaḷ imaikoṭṭātu nōkkiṉārkaḷ. kaḻuvappaṭṭa pūvitaḻ kaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa vaṇaṅkutaṟkuriya teyvavaṭivam vākaippūviṉ mēlum aṟukampulliṉ mēlum vaikkappaṭṭatu. atu pū moṭṭu kaḷālum veṇṇūlālum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭup puṉitamākiya uṭai tarikkappaṭṭatu. maḻaittuḷikaḷ pōṉṟa maṇal cintappaṭṭa pantaliṉ kīḻt talaivi vīṟṟiruntāḷ. āparaṇap poṟaiyiṉāl cōrvaṭainta maṇappeṇṇiṉ viyarvai kāyumpaṭi cuṟṟattāh viciṟiyiṉāl vīci avaḷait talaivaṉukku aḷittārkaḷ. (akam. 136)
palar uṇṭapiṉṉum uḻuntuṭaṉ camaikkappaṭṭa cōṟṟuk kuviyal iruntatu. niraitta kālkaḷiṉmēṟkaṭṭappaṭṭa pantaliṉkīḻp putiya maṇalaip parappiṉārkaḷ. vīṭṭil viḷakkukaḷ ēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. talaivaṉum talaiviyum pūmālaikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭārkaḷ. naṭcattiraṅkaḷ tīya palaṉaik koṭāta nalla vaḷarpiṟaip pakkattē viṭiyaṟkālaiyil cila peṇkaḷ kuṭaṅkaḷait tāṅkavum, maṟṟavarkaḷ putiya akaṉṟa kuṭaṅkaḷai ovvoruvarāka māṟavum, vayatiṉ mutirnta peṇkaḷ perum āravārañ ceytārkaḷ. putalvaraip payanta aḻakiya āparaṇaṅkaḷaiyaṇinta peṇkaḷ pūvitaḻum nellum iṭappaṭṭa nīrai maṇappeṇṇiṉ kariya kūntal pirakācikkumpaṭi peytu, ‘kaṟpu neṟiyil niṉṟu kaṇavaṉukkut tuṇaiyāy vāḻvāyāka’ eṉṟu vāḻttiṉar. maṇaccaṭaṅku niṟaivēṟiya irākkālattilē ayalilē uḷḷa peṇkaḷ kūṭi maṇamakaḷaip putiya āṭai mutaliyavaṟṟāl alaṅkarittut talaiva ṉiṭattuykka avaḷ nāṇattiṉāloṭuṅkiṉāḷ. (akam. 86)
22. pēymakaḷ
kāynta mayiriṉaiyum, nirai ovvāta palliṉaiyum, periya vāyiṉaiyum, kōpattāṟ cuḻalukiṉṟa kaṇṇiṉaiyum koṭiya pārvaiyiṉaiyum, pituṅkiya kaṇṇaiyumuṭaiya kūkaiyōṭē kaṭiya pāmpu tūṅkukaiyiṉālē periya mulaiyai varuttukiṉṟa kātiṉaiyum, kuṟaipāṭuṭaiya uṭaliṉaiyum kaṇṭār añcum naṭaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya veruvarun tōṟṟattaiyuṭaiya pēymakaḷ utirattai yaḷainta kūriya ukiriṉaiyuṭaiya koṭiya viralālē kaṇkaḷait tōṇṭi, uṇṇap paṭṭa mikka muṭaināṟṟattaiyuṭaiya kariya talaiyai oḷi poruntiya vaḷaiyalaṇinta kaiyilē eṭuttu veṟṟik kaḷattaip pāṭit. tōḷai yacaittu, niṇattait tiṉkiṉṟa vāykaḷāyt tuṇaṅkaik kūttāṭuvāḷ.
23. cila nampikkaikaḷ
kākaṅ karaivatu viruntiṉar varuvataṟkaṟikuṟiyeṉak karutappaṭṭatu. puṭkaraital allatu avai paṟakkun ticaikaḷ tām uṉṉic cellum karumaṅkaḷukku anukūlam allatu piratikūlam ēṟpaṭuvatai uṇarttum eṉa nampap paṭṭatu. takka paricil peṟāviṭattup pulavarkaḷ puḷḷaiyum poḻutaiyum paḻippārkaḷ (puṟam-204), pēykaḷ maraṅkaḷilum iṭukāṭu cuṭukāṭukaḷilum uṟaiyum. pēymakaḷir pōrkkaḷaṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu iṟantavarkaḷiṉ kāyaṅkaḷil viralkaḷai aḷaintu avaṟṟāl taṅkaḷ talaiyiṉaik kōtic cenniṟamāy viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ piṇaṅ kaḷait taḻuvip pulāliṉaip pucittārkaḷ. pēykaḷai ōṭṭuvataṟku veṇ kaṭukaip pukaippittal muṉṉuḷḷōr vaḻakku. pēy makaḷir maṉita niṇat tilum irattattilum viruppuḷḷōrāvar. ākavē, avai puṇpaṭṭa pōrvīraraic cūḻntu tiriyum. avai puṇṇait tīṇṭiṉāl puṇ āṟātu. puṇpaṭṭōṉ iṟantu viṭuvāṉ. taṉ kaṇavaṉiṉ puṇṇaip pēy tīṇṭātapaṭi avaṉuṭaiya maṉaivi vēppilai iravam ilaikaḷai vīṭṭiṉ iṟappil ceruki, yāḻ mutaliya icaikkaruvikaḷai olippittu veṇkaṭuku citaṟi, kāñcipāṭi, vācaṉaip poruḷkaḷaip pukaip pippāḷ.
“tīṅkaṉi yiravamoṭu vēmpumaṉaic cerīi
vāṅkumarup piyāḻoṭu palliyaṅ kaṟaṅkak
kaipayap peyarttu maiyiḻu tiḻuki
yaiyavi citaṟi yāmpa lūti
yicaimaṇi yeṟintu kāñci pāṭi
neṭunakar varaippiṟ kaṭinaṟai pukaii” (puṟam-281)
24. talaikkōl
poṉ uṟaiyiṭṭu, muttukkaḷum navamaṇikaḷum aḻutti alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa mūṅkiṟkōl oṉṟu araṅkiṉ mattiyil vaikkap paṭṭiruntatu. im mūṅkiṟkōl perumpālum pakai aracaṉiṉ veṇ koṟṟak kuṭaiyiṉ kāmpāka virukkum. aḵtu araṇmaṉaiyiṉ ōr aṟaiyil vaṇakkattukkuriyatāka vaikkappaṭum. kūttu nikaḻum nāḷil atu poṟkuṭaṅkaḷil niṟaikkappaṭṭa nīrāl muḻukkāṭṭiyapiṉ pūmālaiyālum muttumālaikaḷālum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭup, paṭṭattu yāṉaiyil ēṟṟi, ūrvalañ ceyyappaṭṭu. araṅkiṉ mattiyil nāṭṭap paṭum. āṭṭan toṭaṅkumuṉ kūttiyar talaikkōlai vaṇaṅkutal marapu. talaikkōl kūttiyarkku aḷikkappaṭum paṭṭamumām.
25. parattaiyar
paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷil parattaiyar cērikaḷ iruntaṉa. kalittokaiyil ēṉātippāṭi eṉa ōriṭattiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. iḵtu ēṉātippaṭṭam peṟṟā ṉoruvaṉ ēṟpaṭuttiya parattaiyar cēriyeṉa ataṉuraiyāl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. parattaiyar āṭalpāṭalkaḷiṟ ciṟantu kaṇṇukku maiyeḻutik kaṇṭār mayaṅkumpaṭi tammai alaṅkarittu viḷaṅku vārkaḷ. avarkaḷ āṭavarait tammuṭaṉ ērikaḷilum poykaikaḷilum āṭumpaṭitūṇṭuvārkaḷ. kuṟuntokaiyil oru parattaiyiṉ kūṟṟākak kūṟappaṭuvatu varumāṟu:
nāṅkaḷ āmpaliṉ taḻaiyaic cūṭi nīrāṭac celvōm. avaḷ (maṉaivi) eṅkaḷiṭattil accaṅkoḷvāḷāyiṉ tāṉum taṉatu cuṟṟat tārum eḻiṉiyiṉ paṭaiyaip pōl, avaṉaik kāttukkoḷḷaṭṭum, (kuṟun-80) viḻākkālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ āṭavarai mayakkuvataṟkuk kūṭiṉārkaḷ. “āmpal ilaiyiṉāl alkulai aḻaku peṟa alaṅkarittu viḻāvukkuc celvēṉ. intac ceḻumai mikka nāṭṭiṉ talaivaṉ kāṇpāṉāyiṉ, eṉṉai varikkāmal viṭutal aritākum.” (naṟ-390) maṉaiviyar taṅ kaṇavaraip parattaiyar mayakkik koṇṭu cellātapaṭi kāttārkaḷ.
“maṭakkaṇ takarak kūntaṟ paṇaittōḷ
vārnta vāleyiṟṟuc cērntuceṟi kuṟaṅkiṟ
piṇaiya lantaḻait taiit tuṇaiyilaḷ
viḻavukkaḷam poliya vantuniṉ ṟaṉaḷē
eḻumiṉō veḻumiṉeṅ koḻunaṟ kākkam” (naṟ-170)
“ap pōtaravu pārttirunta parattaiyar kuḻalūti yāḻeḻīit taṇṇumai yiyakki muḻaviyampit talaimakaṉai iṅkuk kūttuṇ ṭeṉpatu aṟivippa.” eṉṉai?
‘kuḻaleḻīi yāḻeḻīit taṇṇumaip piṉṉar
muḻaviyampa lāman tirikai’
eṉṟu kūttunū luṭaiyāruñ coṉṉā reṉpatu. avvakai aṟivik kappaṭṭa talaimakaṉ nām itaṉai oru kāl nōkkip pōtumeṉṟu cellum. ceṉṟakkāl avarkaḷ taṅkaṇ tāḻvippa reṉpatu. (iṟai-akapporuḷurai)
26. caṅka nūlkaḷāl aṟiyum tamiḻar nākarikam
1. pattuppāṭṭāl aṟiyappaṭuvaṉa:
aṟukampullāl tiritta paḻutaiyaittiṉṟa cemmaṟikkiṭāyiṉatu iṟaicci, irumpuk kampiyil kōttuc cuṭṭa iṟaicci, pakalu miravum iṟaicciyait tiṉṟu paṟkaḷ muṉai maḻuṅkutal, nālu kutirai pūṭṭiya tēr, ēḻaṭipiṉ cellal, tēṉākiya neyyōṭē kiḻaṅkaiyum viṟṟavarkaḷ, mīṉiṉatu neyyōṭē naṟavaiyum koṇṭupōtal, vāḻaippūveṉap polinta vōti, umaṇarōṭu vanta manti ciṟu piḷḷaikaḷōṭu kilukilu viḷaiyāṭutal, amiḻtuviḷai tīṅkaṉi oḷavaikkīnta atikaṉ, aravu vekuṇṭaṉṉa naṟavu nalkal, āypāmpu koṭutta nīlappaṭṭāṭaiyai ālamar celvarkkuk koṭuttatu, kaṟṟōṟttuṭutta paṭivappārppāṉ, pāvai viḷakku, periya koṭi pāymarattilē yāṭukiṉṟa marakkalam, puli kūṭṭilē aṭaikkappaṭṭiruttal, māṟupāṭṭaiyēṟṟuk kōṟaṟ ṟoḻilaiyuṭaiya ēṟṟiṉatu cevvittōlai mayir cīvāmaṟ pōrtta muracam olittal, ilaṅku iḻai makaḷir polamkalattu ēntiya maṇaṅ kamaḻ tēṟal maṭuppa makiḻntuṇṭal, irumpuceyviḷakku, kañcitōytta tukil, naṭuniciyil vēppilaiyait talaiyilē kaṭṭiṉa vēlaiyuṭaiya vēntaṉ pācaṟaiyiṟ puṇpaṭṭōraip parikarittal, kāri uṇṭik kaṭavuḷ.
2. akanāṉūṟṟālaṟiyappaṭuvaṉa:
naṭukal vaḻipāṭu, puli, tāṉ koṉṟa yāṉai iṭappakkam vīḻntāl māṉattiṉāl ataṉai uṇṇāmai, makaḷir kuṟiñcip pāṭṭaik kēṭṭut tiṉai mēya vanta yāṉai uṟaṅkuvatu, vēṅkaimalar koyyum makaḷir puli puli eṉṟa oliyaik kēṭṭup puliyai ōṭṭuvataṟku āṭavar villuṅ kompum eṭuttukkoṇṭu vantu puliyaik kāṇāmal veḷkip pōtal, talaivaṉ parattaiyoṭu kāviriyil nīr viḷaiyāṭutal, kaṇavar varavaik karutic cuvariṟ kōṭiṭṭu nāḷ eṇṇutal, peṇ āmaiyīṉṟa muṭṭaiyai āṇ āmai pātukāttal.
3. kuṟantokaiyālaṟiyappaṭuvaṉa:
āviṉ kaḻuttil maṇi yaṇital, makaḷir vaṇṭalayartal, kuṟacciṟār kuṟi collutal, ciṟu piḷḷaikaḷ ciṟutēruruṭṭi viḷaiyāṭutal, pulippaṟṟāli ciṟārk kaṇital, peṇkaḷ tuṇaṅkaikkūttāṭutal, vaṇṇātti kañcippacai pōṭutal, viḷaiyāṭṭu makaḷir maṇal vīṭukaṭṭutal, nuḷaicciyar nel peṟṟu upput tarutal, paratavar kaṭṭu marattiliruntu mīṉ piṭittal, alavaṉ taṉatu peṭaiyai nīrāṭṭutal, aṉṟil paṉaimaṭaliṟ kūṭukaṭṭutal, kuyil mūṅkiliṟ ṟaṅkutal, cūlmakaḷir puḷippai virumputal, vāḷai mīṉukkuk kompiruttal, kiḷi vēppam paḻattaik kavvutal, yāṉai taṉakkuc ceyta tīṅkai maṟavāmai, yāṉai vāḻaittaṇṭāl matamaḻital, tuyarāl varuṅ kaṇṇīrveppamātal.
4. naṟṟiṇaiyālaṟiyappaṭuvaṉa:
kātali cuvarilē kōṭiṭṭup pirinta talaimakaṉ varunāḷ eṇṇu tal, kālāṟ pantu viḷaiyāṭutal, kātalaṉ varavaip palli kūṟutal, irumpu kāycciya ulaiyilē kollaṉ paṉaimaṭaliluḷḷa nīr teḷittu neruppai aṇaittal, erumai mēyppāṉ ataṉmēl ēṟiccellal.
5. kalittokaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum ceytikaḷ:
mukkaṇṇāṉ mūveyilai erittatu (pālai-2), ‘eṟittaru katir tāṅkiyēntiya kuṭainīḻa, luṟittāḻnta karakamu muraicāṉṟa mukkōlu, neṟippaṭac cuvalacaiivēṟōrā neñcattuk, kuṟippēval ceyaṉ mālaik koḷainaṭaiyantaṇar’ (pālai-9) palkalaic cilpūṅ kaliṅkattaḷ (kuṟi-56) taiyil nīrāṭiya tavam (kuṟi-59) ōttuṭai yantaṇaṉ erivalañ ceyvāṉ pōl (maru-66) ‘kiḷarmaṇiyārppārppac cāayc cāyayc cellun taḷarnaṭaic ciṟār’(maru-80), ālamar celvaṉ aṇicāl peruviṟal (81) ālamar celvaṉaṇicāl makaṉ viḻā(83) cuṟāvē ṟeḻutiṉa mōtiram (maru-84), tēraivāyk kiṇkiṇi (maru-86) mukam kutirai mukamum uṭal makkaḷuṭalumākiya iraṇṭu aḻakukkup poruntiya cūrapaṉmāvaikkoṉṟa civanta vēlaiyuṭaiya murukaṉ(93), kuṟumpūḻp pōr (95), toyyil poṟitta vaṉamulai (maru-97), mīṉpūt tavirvaru mantivāṉ vicumpupōl-vāṉpoṟi paranta puḷḷi veḷḷai yuṅ-kolaivaṉ cūṭiya kuḻavittiṅkaḷ pōl-vaḷaipumalinta kōṭaṇi cēy (103), perum peyark kaṇicciyōṉ maṇimiṭaṟṟaṇi pōl (105), neruppukkai toṭṭavar pōla vitirttiṭṭu (115), tampukaḻ kēṭṭār pōṟ ṟalaicāyttu (119), mukkōlko ḷantaṇar mutumoḻi niṉaivār pōl (126), uṇkaṭaṉ vaḻimoḻin tirakkuṅkāṉ mukaṉuntāṅ-koṇṭatu koṭukkuṅkāṉ mukaṉumvē ṟākutal-paṇṭu mivvulakat tiyaṟkai (22), irāvaṇaṉ malaiyai eṭuttal(38), nūṟṟuvar talaivaṉaik kuṟaṅkaṟuttal (52), mallarai maṟañcāytta māl (134) kuṅkiliya meḻutiṉa oḷivirinta toḻilkaḷai yuṭaiyavākiya vāykaḷaiyuṭaiya marattāṟ ceyta ciṟupāvaiyālum pāṉaiyālum ciṟupeṇkaḷ viḷaiyāṭutal (59), kokkurittāṟ pōṉṟa maṭuppaiyuṭaiyavaṉ (94), maraṅkaḷiṟṟeyva muṟaital(101), tuccātaṉaṉai vīmaṉ koṉṟatu (101) cikaṇṭi turōṇaṉaik kollal (101), āycciyar taṅ kaṇavar kaikaḷaik kōttuk kuravai yāṭutal (106) cuṟāviṉatu maruppāṟ ceyta palakaiyaik kōttup puṟavitaḻoṭinta neytaṟ pūvai neṭiya nārilē toṭuttu aḻaku peṟak kaṭṭik kaiyālē vācittalaiyuṭaiya yāḻ(131).
6. cintāmaṇiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum paḻaiya ceytikaḷ:
aruntati kāṭṭal (2669), āviyaṉṉa tukil (67), elimayirp pōrvai (2680), ēṉāti mōtirañ ceṟitta cēṉātipati (2167), kallūri (395), kutiraikaḷiṉ tuṭaikaḷil avai piṟanta iṭappeyarai eḻututal (2215), yāṉaiyaraciṉ valamarup pīrntu ceyta cīppu (2436), muḻantāḷaḷa vāka vīraruṭukku muṭai (468), valampuri vaṭivac ceppu (2475), poṉ ceynāṇiṟ kuñciyaik kaṭṭutal (2288).
7. paripāṭalil kāṇappaṭuñ ceytikaḷ:
intiraṉ cāpamēṟṟatu (19) immaiyiṉ paṅ kuṟittuk kaṉṉiyar tainnīrāṭal (11), irākukētukaḷ kāṇappaṭuvaṉa valla veṉpatu (12), iḷaiya makaḷiruṭaiya aḻukai tīrttaṟkut tāyar puli puli yeṉṟu accuṟuttal (14), nakattilum kaṉṉattilum cempañcu eḻututal (6), paḻantēṉāṟ ceyta tēṟal(16), puṇṇiya natiyil nīrāṭuvōr poṉ mutaliyavaṟṟāl ceyvitta mīṉ mutaliyavaṟṟai atil cellaviṭutal(16), poṉṉāṟ ceyta naṇṭu iṟavu vāḷaikaḷai āṟṟil viṭutal (10), makaḷir tērai makaḷir ōṭṭutal (11), makaḷir piṭiyai ūrtal (10), parattaiyar māvum piṭiyum ūrtal (10).
8. peruṅkataiyil kāṇappaṭum ceytikaḷ:
pacu vaḻipāṭu (1-34-16), piraccōtaṉaṉukkut tēviyar patiṉāṟā yiravar (1-34-77), cītēvi yeṉṉum talaikkōlam (1-34-121), poṉṉā lākiya ciṟupāttiram (1-34-160), talaikkōr peṇṭir (1-35-72), pālāvi pōṉṟa nuṇṇiya āṭai (1-36-64), vipūti (1-36-225), poṟpūṇ kaṭṭiya pirampu (1-37-9), pēy piṭittāṭuvōriṉ piṉ paṟaikkoṭṭic cellal (1-37-246), kaṭikaiyāram (1-38-123), poṟiyoṟṟu (muttirai) (1-38-286) māṉkaṇ pōṉṟa cāḷaram (1-40-9), eṭṭippaṭṭam peṟṟa aracakumaraṉ (1-40-116), koṭivaṭiva meḻutiṉa mārpu (1-40-143), mulaikkaccu (1-40-144), pañcakauviyam(1-40-266), aracaṉukkup piḻaiceytōr tammuṭaiya aḷavuḷḷa poṟpāvaiyoṉṟaic ceyvittu avaṉ ciṉattaip pōkkutal (1-40-371,4) neṟkatirai makaḷir cūṭutal (1-42-68), kāvitip paṭṭattuk kaṟikuṟi yākak koṭukkappaṭum poṟpaṭṭam (1-42-174), kayal vaṭivākac ceyta veṟṟilaippai (1-44-229), pulivaṭivam amainta kālaiyuṭaiya kaṭṭil (1-47-175) elimayirāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pūttoḻilai yuṭaiya pōrvai (1-47-179) teṉṉaṅkaḷḷum tēṉāṟ camaitta kaḷḷiṉ teḷivum (2-2-177), aracaṉ ciṅkācaṉattil vīṟṟiruntu muṭipuṉaiyu miṭattup paṭṭattut tēviyum uṭaṉiruttal (2-4-24), aracilai pōl poṟṟakaṭṭāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa āparaṇam (2-7-73), maṇikaḷ iḻaitta mōtiram (2-12-74), taṉpeyareḻutiṉa mōtiram (3-9-70), pavaḻattāṟ ceyta kumiḻaiyuṭaiya poṉṉālākiya mitiyaṭi (3-22-202), cittiraṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭa uyarnta māṭaṅkaḷ (4-3-35), kaṇavaṉaip pirintuṟaiyum makaḷir kūntal puṉaiyāmai (4-7-103), yāṉaittantattāṟ ceyta veṇpūkkōvaikaḷ (4-14-64), pulimuka māṭam (4-15-108).
9. aiṅkuṟunūṟṟalaṟiyappaṭuvaṉa:
iṭaiyar karumpu kuṇilāk kaṉiyukuttal, iḷamakaḷir tumpai mālaiyaṇital, poymmaippuli kāvalukku vaikkappaṭutal, kuraṅku mūṅkililēṟi viḷaiyāṭutal, curañcelvōr palaviṉ kaṉiyai aruntic cellal, parattaiyait tērēṟṟi varutal, parattaiyarōṭu talaimakaṉ puṉalāṭal, makaḷir iṭaiyil poṟkācaṇital, makaḷir pantāṭutal, makaḷir eri pukutal, mutalai taṉ piḷḷaiyait tiṉṉutal.
10. cilappatikārattilaṟiyappaṭuvaṉa:
antiyil makaḷir malaraiyum aṟukaiyum nellaiyum maṅkalamākac cintutal, āṉaimēṟ paṟaiyaṟaital, iraṇṭāñ cāmattiṟa kaṇṭa kaṉā eṭṭumātattiṟ palikkumeṉpatu, tēvācurayuttam patiṉeṭṭāṇṭilum, irāma irāvaṇa yuttam patiṉeṭṭu mātattilum muṭintateṉpatu. iṟantavarkaḷai uttēcittut tāṉañ ceytal, iṟaivaṉ cūṭiya piṟai iraṇṭu kalaittu eṉpatu, uyirppaliyuṇṇum mayirkkaṇ muracu, eṇṇeṇkalaiyōr (vēcaiyar), eḻunilai māṭam kaṭaiyil iṉṉa carakku īṇṭuḷḷateṉṟu aṟivikkak koṭikaḷeṭuttal, kalaṅkarai viḷakkam, kaṟpuṭaiya makaḷir irukkum nāṭṭil maḻai peyyumeṉpatu, cañcīvi nāṉku vakai -calliya karaṇi, cantāṉa karaṇi, camaṉiya karaṇi, miruta cañcīvi; cantiraṉ kōyil, cūriyaṉ kōyil, kaḷavu nūl, teyvattaip pūvum pukaiyum cāntum kaṇṇiyum koṇṭu vaḻipaṭutal, nūlēṇi, pulippallait tāliyākak kaṭṭutal, potikaḷiṉ mēl aḷavu, eṇ, niṟai poṟikkappaṭṭiruttal, yāṉaip piṭariyil makaḷirai yēṟṟi nakarkku maṇattaiyaṟivittal.
27. maturai
(2000 varuṭaṅkaḷukku muṉ)
“tiru vaḻutināṭeṉṉuñ ciṟappup peyar peṟṟa pāṇṭi maṇṭalam kuṟiñci, mullai, marutam, neytal, pālai eṉap periyōrāl varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭa aivakai nilaṅkaḷum porunti iṉitu viḷaṅkum. kuṟiñci nilattilē iñciyum mañcaḷum miḷakum veṇciṟukaṭukum tōrai nellum aivaṉa nellum vekuvāyp palkiyirukkum. kuṟattiyar tiṉaikkiḷi kaṭiyum ōcaiyum kāṉavar avarai mēyum āmāvaik kaṭikiṉṟa pūcalum, pūkkoyyum makaḷir puli, puli yeṉṟu kūṟumoliyum māṟimāṟi oliyā niṟkum. mullaiyiṉkaṇ eḷḷiḷaṅkāyum varakiṉ katirum orupāl muṟṟiyirukkum. orupāl ciṟutiṉai koyvar. aṅku miṅkumāḻnta kuḻikaḷil irattiṉaṅkaḷ kiṭantu viḷaṅkum. paciya payirkaḷiṉ iṭaṅkaṭōṟum mucuṇṭaippūvum mullaippūvum utirntu kiṭakkum. marutanilattil kaṉikaṭōṟum yāṉai niṉṟāl maṟaiyum paṭiyāka neṟpayir paccaippacēleṉa vaḷarntirukkum. taṭākaṅkaḷil tāmaraiyum nīlōṟpalamum cētāmpalum malarntu naṟumaṇam vīcum. kaḻaṉikaḷilum kuḷaṅkaḷilum maṭukkaḷilum valaiñar mīṉkaḷaip piṭittu avaikaḷaik koṉṟu kuvikkum āravāramum, karumpiṟkiṭṭa ālai yiṭattōcaiyum, kaḷai paṟippiṭattōcaiyum, nellarikiṉṟa kiṇai yiṉ oliyum, āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum nīrāṭukiṉṟa iraiccalum ākāyattē ceṉṟu muḻaṅkum. neytalākiya kāṉaliṉiṭattē muttum vaḷaiyum paratar tanta palvakaip paṇṭaṅkaḷum vilai ceyyappaṭum. yavaṉa mutaliya tēyattiṉar kutirai mutaliyavaṟṟaik koṇarntu koṭuttu, veḷḷuppum tīmpuḷiyum, koḻutta mīṉkaḷait tuṇittu uppiṭṭularttiya karuvāṭukaḷum eṉṟivai pōṉṟavaṟṟaik koṇṭu pōvar. kuṟiñcikkum mullaikkum iṭaiyilēyuḷḷa pālai nilattil, āṟalai kaḷvar vaḻippōvārai varuttā vaṇṇam aracaṉāl ēvappaṭṭa vīrar, ilai vēynta kuṭilil māṉṟōlākiya paṭukkai yuṭaiyarāyp poruntiyiruppar.
“ivvaintiṇaip pāṅkum aḻakuṟappeṟṟup pāṭalkaḷ cāṉṟa naṉṉāṭṭu naṭuvil, tiruvālavāy, tirunaḷḷāṟu, tirumuṭaṅkai, tirunaṭuvūr eṉṉum nāṉmāṭak kūṭal eṉap peyar peṟṟa maturaimā nakaramāṉatu pūmātiṉ aḻakiya mukampōl polivuṟṟirukkum. innakaraic cūḻn tirukkum matil kaṟpaṭaikaḷ palavuḷavāy viṇṇuṟa ōṅka, ataṉaic cūḻnta akaḻiyāṉatu nīlamaṇi pōlum nīraiyuṭaittāy maṇṇuṟa āḻntirukkum. neṭiya nilaiyiṉaiyum tiṇṇiya kataviṉaiyumuṭaiya vāyiliṉ mītamarnta māṭamāṉatu mēkamulāvum malaipōl ōṅki yirukkum. māntarum māvum iṭaiyaṟāmal vaḻaṅkutalāl vāyilāṉatu iṭaiviṭātōṭukiṉṟa vaiyaiyāṟṟiṉaip pōṉṟirukkum. vāyilaik kaṭanta māttirattil, akaṉṟa terukkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ irupuṟattilum cāḷaraṅkaḷ palavamaintaṉavāy uyarntu tōṉṟum vīṭukaḷum, āṟum ataṉ irukaraiyum pōla viḷaṅkum.
“aṅkāṭit teruvilē, kōyilkaḷukku viḻākkaḷai naṭattik kaṭṭiṉa aḻakiya koṭikaḷum, taṇṭattalaivar peṟṟa veṟṟiyiṉ poruṭṭu eṭutta camayakkoṭikaḷum kaṭkaṭaikaḷil kaḷḷiṉ kaḷippu mikutiyaic cāṟṟukiṉṟa koṭikaḷum iṉṉum palavāy vēṟupaṭṭa koṭikaḷum periya malaiyiṭattu aruvikḷa acaiyumāṟupōla acaiyā niṟkum yāṉaiyun tērum puraviyum vīrarum ākiya nāṟperum paṭaikaḷ palkālum varuvaṉavāy mīḷkaiyiṉālē, pū viṟpārum pūmālai viṟpārum cuṇṇam viṟpārum veṟṟilaiyum pākkum cuṇṇāmpuṭaṉē viṟkiṉṟavarkaḷum tam iṉṉuyirañci ēṅkiyiruntu, annāṟpaṭaiyum akaṉṟa piṉṉar varutta nīṅki., māṭamāḷikai kūṭa kōpuraṅkaḷiṉ kuḷir niḻalilē iruntu iḷaippāṟuvārkaḷ. kiḻap peṇṭir paṇṇiyaṅkaḷai naṟumpūvuṭaṉē ēntic ceṉṟu maṉaika ṭōṟum maṉaikaṭōṟum ulāvi viṟpar.
“mālaip poḻutil viḻāk kāṇac celvarkaḷ, paṭṭuṭuttu uṭaivāḷ tarittu mālai tāṅkit tērkaḷiṉ mītēṟik kālāṭkaḷ puṭai cūḻntuvarak kutiraikaḷaic celuttic celvar. at tiruviḻākkaḷaip peṇkaḷ aramiyattalattiṉiṉṟuṅ kāṇpar. aṅkē kaṭṭiya koṭi kaḷiṉ mītu mantamārutam vīcutalāl ammakaḷir mukaṅkaḷāṉavai mēkat tilē maṟaiyuñ cantiraṉaip pōla orukāṟṟōṉṟi orukāl maṟaiyum.
“putta matattiṉarākiya pēriḷampeṇṭir ciṟupiḷḷaikaḷai eṭuttukkoṇṭu, taṅkaṇavarōṭu pūcaikku vēṇṭum pūviṉaiyum tūpaṅkaḷaiyum kaikaḷil ēntic ceṉṟu vaṇaṅkum pauttap paḷḷikaḷ ōriṭattuk kāṇappaṭum. ciṟanta vētaṅkaḷai ōti, yākāti karumaṅkaḷaic ceytu, piraman tāṅkaḷēyāy, vīṭṭiṉ pattai ivvulakilē tāṉē aṭaikiṉṟa piramavittukkaḷiruppiṭam orupāl pulappaṭum. orupāl, mukkāla oḻukkamum mūvulakiṉ ceytiyum muḻutuṇarum camaṇa muṉivarkaḷ uṟaikiṉṟa amaṇap paḷḷikaḷ kaṭpulaṉām. appāl aṟaṅ kūṟavaiyamum, amaiccarirukkaiyum aḻakuṟa viḷaṅkum.
“caṅkuvaḷai kaṭaivārum, maṇikaḷait tuḷaiyiṭuvārum, poṟ kollarum; poṉvāṇikarum, āṭaiviṟpārum, cittirakkārarum piṟaruṅ kūṭi antikkaṭaiyil neruṅki niṟpar. aṟakkūḻccālaiyil palāppaḻam vāḻaippaḻam muntirikaippaḻam mutaliya paḻaṅkaḷaiyum, pākaṟkāy vāḻaikkāy vaḻutuṇaṅkāy mutalāṉa kāykaḷaiyum, ilai kaṟikaḷaiyum, kaṇṭa carukkarait tēṟṟaiyum, iṟaiccikaḷaiyuṭaiya cōṟṟaiyum kiḻaṅkukaḷuṭaṉē pāṟcōṟu pāl mutaliyaṉavaṟṟaiyum koṇṭuvantu paci tākaṅkaḷiṉāl varuntuvōrkkum tarittirarkkum iṭuvārkaḷ. ikkūṟiya palaviṭaṅkaḷilum eḻukiṉṟa ōcaiyāṉatu antikkālattil palvēṟu paṟavaikaḷ kūṭiya iṭattu eḻukiṉṟa ōcaiyiṉ taṉmaiyatākum.
“poḻutu cāyntapiṉ āṭalpāṭal mutaliya palvakait tuṟai kaḷil avvavar poḻutaik kaḻippar. mutal yāmañ ceṉṟapiṉ kaṭai viṟpār kaṭaikaḷaiyaṭaittum, paṇṇiyam viṟpār avaṟṟiṉ pāṅkarum paṭuttuṟaṅkat. tirunāḷiṉkaṇṇē kūttāṭuṅ kūttar aḵtoḻintu tuyil koḷḷa, oli niṟaintaṭaṅkiṉa kaṭalaiyokkap paṭukkaiyilē tuyil koḷvōr iṉitākak kaṇvaḷarvar.. piṉpu vaikaṟaiyāmattil, antaṇar vētam ōtuvar. yāḻōr narampai iṉitākat teṟittu marutattai vācippar. paṇṭam viṟpār tam kaṭaikaḷai meḻukutalaic ceyvar. kaṟpuṭaiya makaḷir tuyiluṇarnteḻuntu,
“kāṭṭuk kaḷaintu kalaṅkaḻīi yillattai
yāppinī reṅkun teḷittuc ciṟukālai
nīrccāl karaka niṟaiya malaraṇintu
illampoliya vaṭuppiṉuḷ tīppeyka”
eṉṟapaṭi akkālattu illattiṟ ceyyat takuvaṉavaṟṟaic ceyyāniṟpar. kōḻic cēvalkaḷ viṭiyaṟkālattai aṟintu kūvā niṟkum. cēvaṟ paṟavaikaḷ tattam pēṭaikaḷai aḻaiyā niṉṟu tīṅkural ceyyum. kūṭṭilē uṟaikiṉṟa karaṭi puli mutaliya valiya vilaṅkukaḷ muḻaṅkāniṟkum. mutuku kāṭṭip pōnta vīrarum veṭṭuṇṭa vīrarum viṭuttayāṉaikaḷum kutiraikaḷum āṉiraikaḷum, tōlvi yaṭainta aracarkaḷ tiṟamaiyākak koṇarnta kalaṅkaḷum, avai pōṉṟaṉa piṟavum araṇmaṉaiyai nōkkic cellum. iṅṅaṉam kaṅkaiyāṟu kaṭaliṟ kalantāṟ pōla, ulakiṟ poruḷkaḷellām maturaiyai aṭaiyum.
“im mānakariṉ kōyilkoṇṭu ceṅkōl celuttum pāṇṭiyaṉ ‘vaikaṟaiyāman tuyileḻuntu kālaik kaṭaṉaik kaḻittuc cantaṉam pūci, mārpilē mālaitāṅkik kaiyilē vīravaḷaiyuṭaṉ kaṇaiyāḻiyaic ceṟittuk, kañciyiṭṭa tukilaiyuṭuttu, ataṉmē laṇiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷai aḻakuṟavaṇintu, kolumaṇṭapattilē vīṟṟiruppāṉ. irunta vaḷavilē, ēṉātippaṭṭa mutaliya ciṟappup peṟṟa paṭaittalaivar avaṉatu veṟṟit tiṟattai vāḻttuvar. piṉṉar aracaṉ tāṉ nāṭunakar peṟumpaṭiyākavum, taṉ veṟṟi ciṟakkumpaṭiyākavum utaviya vīraraiyellāmaḻaittup pāvalaruṭaṉē pāṇar pāṭṭi yaraiyum aḻaittu yāṉai tēr eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa paricaḷippāṉ. aṅṅaṉam pala pala poruḷkaḷaik koṇarntu koṭuttalāl an nāṭṭil vāḻuṅkuṭimakkaḷ celvam perukiyiruppar.”(maturaikkāñci)

nākariṭamiruntē āriyar eḻutteḻutum muṟaiyaik kaṟṟārkaḷ. ākavē, avarkaḷiṉ eḻuttut tēvanākari eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu.-1800 āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻar, (tēvanākari (tēvar nakariṟ pēcappaṭuvatu) eṉpatē tēvanākari yāyiṟṟu eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.)
cumattirāvilē laputōvā eṉṉumiṭattil oru cācaṉam kiṭaittatu. atil caka varuṭam 1010 kuṟippiṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. atu kuṟitta āṇṭu 1088kkuc cari. accācaṉam āyirattaivar eṉṉum kūṭṭat tiṉar koṭutta naṉkoṭaiyaippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟatu. ic cācaṉattāl cumattirāvil ki.pi. 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil potumakkaḷiṉ cācaṉaṅkaḷil tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉpatu veḷiyākiṉṟatu. nām nampak kūṭāta avvaḷavu tūrattil tamiḻatum tamiḻaratumāṉa celvākku iruntirukkiṉṟatu.1

The country of the hills of mud, the land of Mu, was sacrificed; being twice upheaved it suddenly disappeared during the night, the basin being continually shaken by volcanic forces. Being confined, these caused the land to sink and to rise several times and in various places. At last the surface gave way and ten countries were torn asunder and scattered. Unable to stand the force of the convulsions, they sank with their 64,000,000 of inhabitants 8060 years before the writing of this book.

"The puranas relate that Manu, the Lord of the Dravidas divided the country into two halves giving southern portion to his son Yama and the northern where his capital was situated to his beloved daughter Ela. To this day the southern land now submerged under the sea is known to the masses as the dominion ofYama, the Lord of the South. The land of Ela was Kumari nadu ruled and presided by queens. Even today in Malabar, a portion of the Tamilagam, the queen is the owner of the kingdom and the brother who governs the people is her representative. The succession descends in the female line; and women in Malabar are heiresses and owners of property and are treated with greater regard and consideration than men.- Tamilian Antiquary No.1.

2. "Geology and Natural History make it alike certain at a time within the bounds of human knowledge, Southern India did not form part of Asia. A large continent of which this country once formed part has ever been assumed as necessary to account for the different circumstances. The Ceylon Buddhists and the Puranic writers and the local tradition of the west coast all indicate a great disturbance on the point of the Peninsula and Ceylon within recent times." - Topinard
3. India, South Africa and Australia were connected by an Indo oceanic continent in the Permian Epoch." -H.F.Blanford
2. "Peninsular India or the Deccan (literally, the country to the South) is geologically distinct from the Indo-Gangetic Plain and the Himalaya. It is the remains of a former continent which stretched continuously to Africa in the space now, occupied by the Indian Ocean. The rocks of which it is formed are among the Oldest in the world and show no traces of having ever been submerged. In many parts they are overlaid by a sheet of black 'trap' rock or basalt, which flowed over them as molten lava. In the Deccan we are therefore, in the first days of the world. we see land substantially as it existed before the beginning of life. The Indo Gangetic plain stretches without a break from Indus on the west to the Delta of the Ganges on the east, a distance of twleve hundred miles. When the world was still in the making and before the elevation of the Himalaya, the space now occupied by this plain was a sea, the southern shore of this sea was what is peninsular India. With the rise of the Himalaya the sea disappeared and the rivers draining the Himalaya flowed, into the depression bringing with them the silt which is now the soil of the plain.
-People and Problems of India P.23,24- Sir.T.W. Holderness, G.C.B.

from west to east, and in this sea were laid down the marine deposits of the Himalaya.

1. "The Indian Ocean formed a continent which extended from Sunda Islands along the coast of Asia to the east coast of Africa. This large continent is of great importance from being the possible cradle of the human race."
"There are a number of circumstances (Specially chronological facts) which suggest that the primeval home of man was a continent now sunk below the surface of the Indian Ocean which extended along the South of Asia as it is at present (and probably in direct connection with it) towards the east as far as further India and the Sunda Islands, towards the west as far as the Madagascar and the south shores of Africa.
"This large continent of former times Sclatter, an English man has called Lemuria from the monkey like animals which inhabited it and it is at the same time of great importance from being the probable cradle of the human race which in all likelihood here first developed out of anthropoid apes."-Prof. Ernest Haeckel.
"The locality of the origin of the earliest race from recent researches appears to have been on lands now submerged beneath the Indian Ocean-Science of man Australia, Dec.1900."

"Investigations in relation to race show it is by no means impossible that Southern India was once the passage ground by which the ancient progenitors of northern and mediterranean races proceeded to the parts of the globe which they now inhabit. Human remains and traces have been found on the east coast of an age which is indeterminate but quite beyond the ordinary calculation of history. Antiquarian research is only now beginning to find means of supplementing the deficiency caused by the absence of materials constructed or collected by usual historical methods. 1. Their (The Dravidians) settlements in India extent to such prehistoric times that we cannot even feel sure that we regard them as immigrants or, at least, as either conquerors or colonists on a large scale but rather as aboriginal in the sence in which that term is usually understood.
The hypothesis, that would represent what we know of their history most correctly, would place their original seat in the extreme south, somewhere probably not far from Madura or Tanjore and thence spreading fan like towards the north. They have no traditions which point to any seat of race out of India, or of their having migrated from any country whose inhabitants they can claim any kindred so far as they know they are indigenous and aboriginal.-Dr.Fergusson quoted in Tamilian Antiquary No.1

1. The marvellous uniformity of the flood legends of all parts of the globe, alone remains to be dealt with. Whether these are some varctic version of the story of the lost Atlantis and the submergence or whether they are echoes of a great cosmic parable once taught and held in reverence in some common centre whence they have reverberated throughout the world, does not immediately concern us. Sufficient for our purpose it is to show the universal acceptation of these legends.
"It would be needless waste of time and space to go over these flood stories one by one, suffice to say, that in India, Chaldea, Babylon Media, Greece, Scandinavia, China among the Jews and the Keltic tribes of Britain the legend is absolutely identical in all essentials. Now turn to the west and what do we find? The same story presented in its every detail-presented among the Mexicans (each tribe having in its version) the people of Guatemala, Honduras, Peru and amongst every tribe of North American Indians; It is puerile to suggest that mere coincidence can account for this fundamental identity - The story of Atlantis - W.Scott Elliot p.16.

1. The enthnic type of Sumerians, so strongly masked in their statures and reliefs, was so different from those of the races which surrounded them, as was their language from those of the Semitics, Aryans or others. The face type of the average Indian of today is no doubt much the same as that of his Dravidian race ancestors thousands of years ago. Among the modern Indians, as amongst the modern Greeks or Italians the ancient pre-Aryan type of the land has (as the primitive of the land always does) survived, while that of the Aryan conqueror died out long ago and it is to this Dravidian ethinic type of India that the Sumerians bear most resemblance, so far as we can judge from his monuments. He was very like a southern Hindu of the Dekkan (who still speaks Dravidian languages) and it is by no means impossible that the Sumerians were an Indian race which passed certainly by land, perhaps also by sea through Persia to the valley of the two rivers. It was in the Indian Home (perhaps the Indus Valley that their writing may have been invented and progressed from a purely pictorial to a simplified and abbreviated from which after wards in Babylonia took on it peculiar uniform appearance owing to its being written within a square ended stilus on soft clay. On the way they left the seeds of culture in Elam.. there is little doubt that India must have been one of the earliest centres of civilization and it seems natural to suppose that the strange unsemitic, un-Aryan people who came from the east to civilise the west were of Indian origin, specially when we see with our eyes how very Indian the sumerians were in type. 1. 'Telugu is one of the great Dravidian languages. The word is probably derived from Trilinga (the threelingas of Siva) a name for the old Kingdom of Andhra. It was at one time called by Europeans 'Gentoo' from a Portuguese word meaning gentle. The Telugu-speaking people are partly subjects of Nizam of Hyderabad and partly under British rule beginning north of Madras city, extending north-west to Bellary where Telugu meets Kanarese and north-east to near Orissa. They are taller and fairer than Tamils, otherwise they are of typical Dravidian features. They are of an enterprising people, good farmers and skillful seamen...In 1901 the number of speakers in Telugu in India was nearly 21millions. - Encyclopaedea Britannica.
1. Malayalam a language of Dravidian family spoken on the west coast of Southern India. It is believed to have developed out of Tamil as recently as the 9th century. It possesses a large literature in which words borrowed from Sanskrit are conspicuous. In 1901 the total number of speakers of Malayalam in all India was just about six millions.- Encyclopedia Britannica.
1. "Kanarese, language of Dravidian family spoken by about ten millions of people in South India chiefly in Mysore, Hyderabad and the adjoining districts of Madras and Bombay. It has an ancient literature written in an alphabet closely resembling that employed for Telugu. Since the 12th Century the Canarese speaking people have largely adopted the Lingayat form of faith which may be described as an anti-Brahmanical sect of Siva worshippers; most of them are agriculturists but they also engage actively in trade.-Encyclopaedea Britannica.
2. "Telugu does not take its available literature much anterior to the eleventh centuryA.D. and this literature seems to be modelled upon Sanskrit entirely. Kanarese has certainly a more ancient literature. A work of the 9th century undoubtedly is the Kaviraja Marga of Nirpatungu. Malayalam seems to have grown out of Tamil in the early centuries of the Christian era." - Prof. S.Krishnaswami Aiyangar.
3. Tulu or Tuluva, a language of the Dravidian family,found chiefly in the South Kanara District of Madras. It has no literature, nor it has been adopted for
official use even where it is spoken by the majority of the population. In 1901 the total number of speakers of Tulu exceeded half a million."-Encyclopaedea Britannica.
1. Singhalese is essentially, Dravidian language. Its evolution too seems to have been on a Tamil basis and so we seem safe in saying that, while in regard to its word development. Singhalese is the child of Pali and Sanskrit: it is with regard to its physical features and structure essentially the daugthter of Tamil. with regard to the laws governing the relation of words in a sentence, viz. the laws of syntax, including idioms we find great many laws which cannot be explained except in the principle of Tamil grammar. - Mudaliar W.F.Gunawardhana

1. “Dialects of the same family of languages were spoken throughout India, except in the Vindhyan regions in the Neolithic Age: and that is what has been called the Dravidian family. The distinction between the spoken dialects of North India, to which the name Gaudian has been given by modren scholars and which have been held to be degenerations of Sanskrit or of Prakrit, and those of Southern India, to which the name Dravidian has been given, is I hold , a distinction without a difference, except that the North Indian dialects have been very much more profoundly affected by Sanskrit than those of South India. The Neolithians of North India spoke languages of their own which, I hold, were structurally allied to the so called Dravidian family of languages and not to Sanskrit or Prakrit" (Stone age in India, p.43.)
