"The Hindu theory that mankind is divided into four Varnas, or groups of castes-Brahmana, Kshatriya , Vaisya and Sudra was wholly foreign to the southerners" "Dravidian culture"- The Brahamanical ideas and institution although universally diffused in every province have not been wholly victorious. Prehistoric forms of worship and many utterly un-Aryan social practices survive specially in the peninsula among that people speaking Dravidian languages. We see there the strange spectacle of any exaggerated regard for caste coexisting with sorts of weird notions and customs allied to Brahman traditions. Where it is praobable that the Dravidian civilization is even older than the Indo-Aryan Brahmanical culture of the north which was long regarded in the south as an unwelcome intruder to be resisted strenuously."
"The amount of Aryan blood in the people to the south of Narbada is extremely small in fact negligible." (Oxford History of India-Vincent Smith. C.I.E.)
1. iyamam: kollāviratam, meymmai kūṟal, kaḷḷāmai, piṟarporuṭ kātaliṉmai, iv vayiṉintiyam aṭakkalum. iyamam (tivākaram)
niyamam: tavamoṭu tūymai, tattuvanū lōtal, maṉamuvan tiruttal, teyvam vaḻipaṭal, niṉaiyuṅkālai niyamamākum. (tivākaram)
1. vētattil varuṇaṉ acuraṉ eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṟāṉ. acurar tamiḻarē yeṉpatu varalāṟṟu nūlār kaṇṭa muṭipu.
1. The vedic culture which resembles that of the Homeric Greeks or the Celtic Irish at the beginning of the Christian era, or that of the pre-Christian Teutons and slaves becomes transformed in the epics into the Hindu culture through the influence of Dravidians. The Aryan idea of worship during the earliest period was to call on the farther sky or some other shining one to look from on high on the sacrificer, and receive from him the offerings of fat or flesh: cakes and drink. But soon Puja or worship takes the place of Homa or sacrifice. Image worship which was a striking feature of the Dravidian faith was accepted by the Aryans. The ideals of vegetarianism and non violence (Ahimsa) also developed. The Vedic tradition has dominated by the Agamic and today Hindu culture shows the influence of the Agama as much as that of the Vedas. The Aryan and the Dravidian do not exist side by side in Hinduism " Contact with the highly civilized Dravidians led to the transformation of Vedism into a theistic religion." (Sir Radhakrishnan)
2. "It need not be thought that the Aryan was always the superior force. There are occasions when the Aryan yielded to the non Aryan and rightly too the epic relates the manner in which the different non-Aryan ones Krishna's struggle with Indra, the prince of the Vedic gods is one instance. The rise of the cult of Siva is another." (Sir Radhakrishnan)

1. The Hymns of Rig Veda- The hymns which are collected in this work are 1028 in number and were composed during several centuries. They are only the mate-rials we have for the history of this early period, which is called the Vedic Age.
“The people of India recognised four Vedas, and this is how they have grown up, some of the hymns by an ancient custom chanted at sacrifices instead of being recited and a separate collection was made of these chanted hymns and called Sama Veda. Special sacrificial formulas and rules also existed from ancient times for the performance of rites, and these formulas and rules were collected under the form of Yajur Veda and a collection of the latter hymns often consisting of charms and incantations against evil influences grew up under the name of Atharva Veda." - The civilization of India by Romesh C.Dutt, C.I.E.
1. The great Appaya Dikshithar horrified at the sight of the slaughtered animals at a vedic sacrifice he performed is said to have cried out,"Oh Ye Vedas! I believe you." (Tamilian Antiquary No.2)
"But there is evidence of its (bulls) having been killed in sacrifices and its cocked flesh offered to the Gods, specially to Indra who seemed to have developed a keen taste and inordinate desire for it." (Rig Vedic, India.)
1. In the Upanishads however we find not only kings but also women and even people of dubious descent taking active part in the literary and philosophical aspirations and often possessors of the highest knowledge...... In the law books it is again and again emphasised that only the Brahman may teach the Vedah and only a member of the three higher classes may be instructed in the Veda. In the Upanishads we are repeatedly told that kings or warriors are in possession of the highest knowledge and the Brahman go to them for instuction... the king impart the doctrine to him and it is the doctrine of transmigration which here, for the first time it appears clearly and distinctly proves to be a doctrine which enacted from warrior class and was originally foreign to the Brahman Theology - History of Indian Literature , P. 220-M.Winternitz. Ph.D.

"They had and still have their own terms pertaining to agriculture, anatomy architecture, astronomy, commerce, domestic economy , family relations , fauna and flora, language and literature, medicine, minerals, politics, religion, war, weight and measures, etc, all of course in their primitive stage. nāṟu,cey, ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, kai, kāl, māṟukōḷ, nel, pāl, muṟṟam, maccu, tāy, appaṉ, teṟku, tāḻai, vali, pūcai, eḻuttu, col, pā, tiṇai, nō, vali, veḷḷi, poṉ, iṟai, ūr, kaṭavuḷ, ampu, vil, mā, kaḻañcu are all pure Tamil words.
"nāṭu kūṟṟam, kōṭṭam as administrative divisions of a country, nellāyam, kārttikaip paccai, kiṉmai, taṟiyiṟai, cekkiṟai, makaṉmai etc,. as names of public taxes amāttiyam, vāriyam, kāvitimai, campiriti etc, as official terms, kuṟuṇi, patakku, tūṇi, muntiri, kāṇi, kaḻañcu and other words of native weight and measures... kācu, paṇam, tuṭṭu, varākaṉ and other enominations of old coinage.
1. "It was from the Nagas that the Aryas first learnt the art of writing; and hence Sanskrit characters are to this day known as Deva-nagari. (Tamils 1800 year ago. p.45)
"At Laboe Toewa, Paros, Sumatra has been found a Tamil inscription dated in the Saka year 1010-A.D. 1088. It records a gift by a body of persons who are styled the one-thousand five hundred. This epigraph proves that the Tamil language was used in public documents on the Island of Sumatra in the 11th Century A.D. accordingly, the influence which the Tamils and their language exercised is much greater than the traditional boundaries would warrant us to believe. - Rai Bahadur V.Venkayya, M.A.,
- Epigraphist to the Government of India.

7. araca marapukaḷ
1. cōḻar maṉṉar
muṭittalaik kōpperunaṟkiḷḷi
vēṟpaḵṟaṭakkaip peruviṟaṟkiḷḷi uruvappaḵṟēr iḷañcēṭceṉṉi
(neṭuñcēralātaṉōṭu porutu tuñciyavaṉ) (aḻuntūr vēḷiṉ makaḷai maṇantavaṉ)
karikāṟcōḻaṉ (ki.pi.50-95) maṟupeyar tirumāvaḷavaṉ; nāṅkūrvēḷiṉ makaḷai maṇantavaṉ; ivaṉ makaḷ cōṇai (maṇakkiḷḷi) cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ tāy.
(1)cēralātaṉ=cōṇai (cēṭceṉṉi nalaṅkiḷḷi) māvaḷattāṉ neṭuṅkiḷḷi
(vāṉavarmaṉ) (ki.pi.95-105) (kāriyāṟṟiṟ ṟuñciyavaṉ)
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ
(maṟupeyar, (cilappatikāra āciriyar)
imayavarampaṉ
kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ (ki.pi.105-120) perunaṟkiḷḷi (ki.mu.120-150)
pīlivaḷaiyai maṇantavaṉ; kuḷamuṟṟattut tuñciyavaṉ, (irāca cūyam vēṭṭavaṉ)

xvi
tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷiṉ vāḻvum toṇṭum (1893 - 1967)
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ toṉmaiyai ataṉ taṉit taṉmaiyai ulaka moḻikaḷōṭu oppiṭṭu virivāka āyvu ceytavar tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷ. tamiḻ iṉattiṉ mēṉmaiyai varalāṟṟu nōkkil āyvu ceytavarum avarē.
‘toṇṭu ceyvāy! tamiḻukkut
tuṟaitōṟum tuṟaitōṟum tuṭitteḻuntē’
eṉpār puraṭcik kaviñar pāratitācaṉār. puraṭcik kaviñariṉ kaṉavai niṉaivākkum vakaiyil, tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷ tamiḻiṉ tuṟaitōṟum tuṟaitōṟum ariya āyvu nūlkaḷai eḻutiyavar. tamatu nūlkaḷiṉ vāyilāka vīḻcciyuṟṟa tamiḻiṉattai eḻucci peṟac ceytavar.
na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷiṉ tamiḻp paṇiyō malaiyiṉum māṇap periyatu. āyiṉum avaruṭaiya vāḻkkaip pativukaḷāka namakkuk kiṭaippaṉa tiṉaiyaḷavē yākum. tamiḻarkaḷ anta māmaṉitariṉ tamiḻp paṇiyaik kūrntu aṟintu takka vakaiyil avaruṭaiya vāḻkkai varalāṟṟaip pativu ceyyat tavaṟi viṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟutāṉ tōṉṟukiṟatu. iṉṟu avaruṭaiya vāḻkakaik kuṟippukaḷāka namakkuk kiṭaippaṉa mikac cilavākum. avai:-
tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷ īḻa nāṭṭil kantarōṭai eṉṉum ūril 1893 ām āṇṭil tiru naṉṉiyar ciṉṉattampi eṉpāriṉ putalvarāyp piṟantār. takka āciriyariṭam payiṉṟu kalviyil tērntār. piṉṉar kantarōṭai eṉṉum ūril uḷḷa oru paḷḷiyil āciriyarākap paṇi purintār. āciriyar paṇiyilirukkum pōtē takka perum pulavarkaḷait tēṭic ceṉṟu tamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ muṟaiyākap payiṉṟu perum pulavarāy viḷaṅkiṉār. āṅkila moḻiyilum tērcci peṟṟa vallunarāyt tikaḻntār. piṉṉar malēciyā nāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu ciṟitukālam pukaivaṇṭi aluvalakattil paṇiyāṟṟiṉār.
na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷaiyavarkaḷiṉ tamiḻaṟivu āḻamum akalamum koṇṭatu. avaratu vāḻkkai muḻumaiyum tamiḻ āyvup paṇiyē perum paṇiyāka amaintatu. avar peṟṟa āṅkila aṟiviṉ tuṇaiyāl tamiḻ moḻi, tamiḻiṉam toṭarpāṉa mēlai nāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷai yellām nuṇukik kaṟṟār viyakkattakka kalvik kaṭalāy viḷaṅkiṉār. aṟupatukku mēṟpaṭṭa āyvu nūlkaḷai eḻutiṉār.
tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷ tām eḻutiya nūlkaḷaip patippittu veḷiyiṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu vēṭkai koṇṭār. ilaṅkaiyil ataṟkup pōtiya vacati illai. ātalāl tamiḻnāṭṭukku varukai purintār. ceṉṉaiyil “oṟṟumai nilaiyam” eṉṉum patippakattiṉ urimaiyāḷarāyt tikaḻntavar vīrapāku piḷḷai eṉpavar. avar na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷ cilavaṟṟai veḷiyiṭṭār. piṉṉar muttamiḻ nilaiyam, āciriyar nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, tirunelvēli caiva cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ vāyilāka na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷaiyavarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷ veḷivaralāyiṉa.
na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷaiyavarkaḷiṉ arumait tuṇaiviyār irattiṉammā eṉappaṭupavar. ivarukkut tirunāvukkaracu eṉṟoru makaṉum maṅkaiyarkkaraci eṉṟoru makaḷārum uṇṭu. tuṇaiviyār pala āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē kālamāṉār. tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻiṉam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ mēṉmaikkāka allum pakalum ayarātu pāṭupaṭṭa tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai tamatu eḻupattu nāṉkām vayatil 1967il ilaṅkaiyil maṟaintār. eṉiṉum tamiḻ vāḻum varai avaratu pukaḻ eṉṟeṉṟum nilaittu niṟkum.
tamiḻaṟiñar kantaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷiṉ tamiḻppaṇi matippu mikkatu; kālattai veṉṟu nilaittuniṟka vallatu. tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻnāṭu, tamiḻppaṇpāṭu, tamiḻiṉam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ varalāṟṟai eḻutiyatil avarukkup perum paṅkuṇṭu. avaruṭaiya āyvup paṇiyaic ciṟitu nōkkuvōm.
akarātip paṇi
tamiḻ moḻiyil mutaṉ mutalil tōṉṟiya akarāti vīramā muṉivar eḻutiya caturakarātiyē yākum. piṉṉar palvēṟu akarātikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa, vaḷarntaṉa, veḷivantaṉa. akarāti vaḷarccip paṇiyil kantaiyā piḷḷaiyavarkaḷukkum perum paṅkuṇṭu. avar paṭaitta akarātikaḷ aintu. 1. centamiḻ akarāti, 2. tamiḻ ilakkiya akarāti, 3. tamiḻp pulavar akarāti, 4. tirukkuṟaḷ akarāti, 5. kālak kuṟippu akarāti eṉpaṉa avai.
na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷ centamiḻ akarāti muṉṉurai yil “nām tamiḻt tāy moḻikkuc ceyyum paṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟāka in nūlaic ceytu muṭittōm” eṉkiṟār. “nūloṉṟaic ceytu tamiḻulakukku utava vēṇṭum eṉṉum āvalāl palavakaiyil muyaṉṟu in nūlaic ceytu muṭittōm” eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. tamiḻ ilakkiya akarātiyil akattiyar mutal vairākkiya tīpam īṟāka āyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻp pulavar akarātiyil pala nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tamiḻppulavarkaḷaip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avar tokutta tirukkuṟaḷ coṟ poruḷ akarāti payaṉpāṭu mikkatu. kālakkuṟippu akarāti putumai yāṉatu. tamiḻ moḻiyil itu pōṉṟa akarāti ituvarai veḷivanta tillai “innūl ōr aṟivuk kaḷañciyam” eṉkiṟār muṉaivar. mā. irāca māṇikkaṉār.
ilakkiyap paṇi
pulavarkaḷukku maṭṭumē payaṉpaṭṭu vanta tamiḻ ilakkiyac celvattai eḷiya makkaḷum paṭittup payaṉ peṟumāṟu urainaṭaiyil vaḻaṅkiya vaḷḷal kantaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷ.
pattuppāṭṭu, akanāṉūṟu, kalittokai, patiṟṟuppattu, paripāṭal, kaliṅkattupparaṇi, puṟapporuḷ viḷakkam, viṟaliviṭutūtu pōṉṟa ilakkiyac celvaṅkaḷai ellām iṉiya eḷiya naṭaiyil urainaṭaiyil tantavar kantaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷ. tirukkuṟaḷukkum, nītineṟi viḷakkattiṟkum ariya urai varaintavar.
tamiḻmoḻi - tamiḻiṉam
tamiḻmoḻi - tamiḻiṉam toṭarpākap patiṉaintukkum mēṟpaṭṭa āyvu nūlkaḷai eḻutiyavar kantaiyā piḷḷai. tamiḻakam, tamiḻ intiyā, tamiḻar carittiram, tamiḻaryār?, varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻantamiḻar, namatumoḻi, namatunāṭu, tamiḻ ārāycci, tamiḻ viḷakkam, muccaṅkam, akattiyar, cintuveḷi nākarikam, tamiḻar paṇpāṭu, tamiḻarcamayam etu? civaṉ, caiva camaya varalāṟu, tamiḻp paḻamaiyum putumaiyum pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa ariya āyavu nūlkaḷaip paṭaittavar. āyirak kaṇakkāṉa nūṟkaṭaluḷ mūḻki eṭutta ariya koṟkai muttukkaḷ avaratu nūlkaḷ. ettaṉai āṇṭukālap pēruḻaippu! niṉaittāl malaipput tōṉṟum. tamiḻiṉ - tamiḻiṉattiṉ toṉmaiyait tamiḻartam paṇpāṭṭai - nākarikattai, tamiḻartam uyar vāḻviyalai ulakaṟiyac ceyta pēraṟiñar kantaiyā piḷḷai.
tirāviṭa nākarikamum - āriyattāl viḷainta kēṭum
toṉmai mikka tirāviṭa nākarikattiṉ ciṟappukaḷai viḷakkum vakaiyil pala nūlkaḷai eḻutiṉār. avai:-
tirāviṭar nākarikam, tirāviṭam eṉṟāl eṉṉa? tirāviṭa intiyā, teṉṉintiyak kulaṅkaḷum kuṭikaḷum pōṉṟa ariya āyvu nūlkaḷai eḻutiṉār. āriyarkaḷāl viḷainta kēṭukaḷ kuṟittum, āriyar tamiḻar kalappu, āriyattāl viḷainta kēṭu, purōkitar āṭci, irāmāyaṇam naṭanta kataiyā? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa nūlkaḷai eḻutiṉār.
piṟa
māṇavar tam aṟivai virivu ceyyum vakaiyil eṭṭu nūlkaḷum, makaḷirukkup pallāṇṭuk kālamāka iḻaikkappaṭṭu varum koṭumaikaḷ kuṟittu mūṉṟu nūlkaḷaiyum eḻutiṉār. mēlum moḻipeyarppu nūlkaḷ cilavum maṉita iṉattōṟṟam kuṟitta nūlkaḷ cilavum eṉap pala nūlkaḷai eḻutiyuḷḷār. puraṭcik kaviñar kūṟiyavāṟu tuṟaitōṟum, tuṟaitōṟum eṇṇaṟṟa nūlkaḷai eḻutit tamiḻukku vaḷam cērttavar. na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai. puṭcikkaviñar pāratitācaṉār, tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷaip paṟṟip piṉvarumāṟu pārāṭṭukiṟār.
“na.ci. kantaiyā eṉum nallavaṉ, vallavaṉ tamiḻ tamiḻiṉa varalāṟaṉaittaiyum tolporuḷ āyviṉ tokai vakai, virittu nilanūl, kaṭalnūl cāṉṟukaḷ niṟaittum tamiḻ nūṟ cāṉṟukaḷ muṭṭaṟut tiyampiyum ilakkiya ilakkaṇac cāṉṟukaḷ koṭuttum paḻakka vaḻakka oḻukkam kāṭṭiyum vaiyakam viyakka varalāṟu eḻutiṉāṉ. poy akaṉṟu meyk kai uyarntatu.”
vāḻka! na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷaiyiṉ perum pukaḻ!
tamiḻaṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷiṉ varalāṟē tamiḻar kaḷāl maṟakkappaṭṭu viṭṭatu. tamiḻp pakaivarkaḷāl maṟaikkappaṭṭu viṭṭatu. aṅṅaṉamirukka am māmaṉitariṉ āyvu nūlkaḷ maṭṭum eṅṅaṉam kiṭaikkum? āḻkaṭaliliruntu muttukkaḷ eṭuppatu pōla, taṅkac curaṅkattiliruntu taṅkattai veṭṭi eṭuppatu pōla, perumuyaṟci eṭuttu na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷait tēṭiṉēṉ. nūlnilaiyaṅkaḷait tēṭittēṭi eṉ kālkaḷ alainta vaṇṇam iruntaṉa. ataṉ viḷaivāka aimpatu nūlkaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. perum putaiyalait tēṭi eṭuttatu pōl perumakiḻvuṟṟēṉ. 
aṉpaṉ
kō. tēvarācaṉ
akam nutalutal
ulakil vāḻum māntar aṉaivarkkum uḷḷārnta eṇṇa ōṭṭaṅkaḷ alai alaiyāy eḻuntu palvēṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷil veḷip paṭukiṉṟaṉa. caṅkak kālattut tamiḻ māntar tamatu eṇṇaṅkaḷai oḻuṅku paṭutti oru varaiyaṟaikkuḷ kaṭṭuppaṭutti vāḻa muṟpaṭṭataṉ viḷaivē nākarikattiṉ toṭakkam eṉalām.
ulakil tōṉṟi vāḻntu varum ellā uyirkkum iṉpam eṉpatu iyalpāka virumpi ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭa oṉṟākavē uḷḷatu. anta iṉpattukakut taṭaiyō iṭaiyīṭō nēriṉ ataip pōkkik koḷḷa muyalum muṟaiyil maṉitak kulattukkut taṉip paṇpu ciṟappāka veḷippaṭṭiruppataic caṅka ilakkiyattiṉ vaḻi naṉku aṟiya muṭikiṟatu.
tolkāppiyattil eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉa mūṉṟu perum pirivāka maṉita vāḻviṉ iyalpai vakuttu ilakkaṇam ceytālum, avaṉ uḷḷattil eḻum uṇarvai veḷippaṭutta moḻiyum collum ataṉ poruḷum oṉṟaiyoṉṟu piṉṉip piṇaintirukkiṉṟa pāṅku puṟattiṇaic ceyyuḷaik kāṭṭilum akattiṇaic ceyyuḷkaḷilēyē mikuntiruppataik kāṇa muṭikiṟatu.
tolkāppiyar ellāc collum poruḷ kuṟittaṉavē eṉṟār. kuṟittaṉavē eṉṉum tēṟṟēkāram poruḷ kuṟiyāta col illai eṉpataiyē uṇarttukiṟatu. iṟṟai nāḷil nammil cilar vañcaka eṇṇattuṭaṉum palar makkaḷiṉ cintaṉai ōṭṭattait tūṇṭi nalvaḻip paṭuttavum uḷḷoṉṟu vaittup puṟamoṉṟu pēcuvataik kēṭkiṟōm.
erutunōy kākkaikkut teriyātu eṉṟu kūṟum pōtum kutiraikkuk kompu muḷaittatu pōlattāṉ eṉṟu kūṟum pōtum (muyaṟkompē) ac colliṉ poruḷaiyum ataṉāl nuṇṇuṇar vuṭaiyār aṟiyum vēṟu poruḷaiyum ac col uṇarttuvatāka aṟiya muṭikiṟatu. viṭukataikaḷ colli aṟivait tūṇṭic cintikka vaippatum cilēṭaiyākap pēci uṭporuḷai uṇarat tūṇṭuvatum iṉṟum naṭaipeṟṟuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟa aṟivārnta ceyalkaḷ.
iyal icai nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻp piriviṉ muṭivum maṉita ṉiṉ uḷḷuṇarvai veḷippaṭutta muyaṉṟa muyaṟciyē. akam - puṟam eṉṟa pirivil uḷḷattu uṇarvaittāṉ nukarntāṉ eṉpatai evvāṟu veḷippaṭuttuvatu? evvāṟu aṟivatu? aṟamō maṟamō - uyir uṭal vēṟupāṭukaḷāl, moḻiyāl, icaiyāl, aḻukaiyāl, caikai eṉṉum nāṭakattāl allavō veḷippaṭutta muṭiyum. akattil eḻum kātal uṇarvai oruvaṉum oruttiyum nukarnta nukarcciyai ittakaiyatu eṉṟu piṟarkku aṟivuṟutta iyalātu. akattāl maṭṭumē uḷ mukamāka nāṭi ārāyntu aṟintu koḷḷamuṭiyum eṉpatu uṇmai eṉṟālum. ivvuṇarvu maṉitak kulattiṟku ottiruppatāl cila pala kuṟippukaḷai maṭṭumē veḷippaṭuttiṉāl pōtum maṟṟavarum aṟintu itu ivvāṟu irukkum eṉṟu uṇarntu makiḻavum oruvarkku oruvar utavi ceyyavum ētuvāka irukkiṟatu.
eṭṭuttokai nūlkaḷuḷ akanāṉūṟu, kuṟuntokai, naṟṟiṇai ākiya mūṉṟum nāṉūṟu nāṉūṟu pāṭalkaḷālāṉa tokai nūlkaḷ. caṅkakkālac cāṉṟōriṉ ariya muyaṟciyāl ivvāṟu tokukkap paṭṭālum akap poruḷ tiṇaik kaḷaṅkaḷ aintum ivaṟṟuḷ kalantuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl kuṟuntokai 4 mutal 8 aṭikaḷum, naṟṟiṇai 9 mutal 12 aṭikaḷum, akanāṉūṟu 13 mutal 21 aṭikaḷum koṇṭa aṭivaraiyaṟai kaḷaiyuṭaiyaṉa. ēṉō akanāṉūṟu nīṇṭa āciriyap pāvāṉ amaintu neṭuntokai eṉap peyar peṟṟālum kaḷiṟṟi yāṉai nirai, maṇimiṭaip pavaḷam, nittilak kōvai eṉa mup pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. uyir eḻuttukaḷ 12 mey eḻuttukaḷ 18 eṉpatāl eḻuttukaḷ ovvoṉṟaṟkum pattup pattākak kaḷiṟṟu yāṉai nirai 12 x 10 = 120 pāṭalkaḷākavum maṇimiṭai pavaḷam 18 x 10 = 180 pāṭalkaḷākavum pirittaṉarō eṉṟu eṇṇat tōṉṟukiṟatu. mataṅ koṇṭa yāṉai pōṉṟa aimpulaṉaiyum oḻuṅku paṭuttak kaḷiṟṟuyāṉai nirai eṉṟu uyir paṉṉiraṇṭai 120 ākap pakuttaṉarō! maṇipōṉṟa meyyāṉa uṭalai niralpaṭa mālaiyākat toṭukkappaṭṭatāka eṇṇip patiṉeṭṭai 180 maṇimiṭai pavaḷamākat tokuttaṉarō! uḷḷamākiya kaṭaliṉ āḻattiliruntu cēkaritta nittilattai muḻumai peṟṟa mālaiyākat tarittu makiḻavō muḻuvatum nūṟi eḻunta veṟṟi yiṉpattaik kuṟikkavō 100 nūṟu pāṭalkaḷai nittilak kōvai eṉat tokuttaṉar eṉṟu eṇṇat tōṉṟukiṟatu. inta muppāṉ piriviṟkuk kāraṇam muḻumaiyākat tōṉṟavillai.
vakupaṭāmal oṟṟaip paṭaiyāy eñci niṟkum eṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya pāṭalkaḷ uripporuḷāl pālait tiṇaip pāṭalkaḷākavum, iraṇṭum eṭṭum urip poruḷāl puṇarcciyai uṇarttum kuṟiñcit tiṇaip pāṭalkaḷākavum, nāṉkil muṭiyum eṇṇuḷḷa pāṭalkaḷ nāṉku uṟutip poruḷkaḷai eṇṇi urip poruḷāl āṟṟi irukkum mullait tiṇaip pāṭalkaḷākavum, āṟāvatu eṇṇil muṭiyum pāṭalkaḷ toṭarntu cellum āṟupōla maṉam orunilaip paṭāmal māṟi māṟi uṭal koḷḷavāyppāka amaintu nīr vaḷam mikka marutattiṇaip pāṭalākavum, muḻumai peṟṟa oṉṟōṭu cuḻiyaic cērttatu pōṉṟa pattām eṇṇuḷḷa pāṭalkaḷ yāṉ eṉ talaivaṉōṭu cērntu eṉṟu muḻumai peṟuvōṉō eṉṟu iraṅki ēṅkum urip poruḷāl neytal tiṇaip pāṭalākavum pakuttut tokuttirukkum pāṅku akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷil maṭṭumē kāṇappaṭum ciṟappākum. mullai, marutam, neytal ākiya mut tiṇaip pāṭalkaḷ ovvoṉṟum camamāka nāṟpatu pāṭalkaḷaik koṇṭu potuvāka amaintuḷḷatu. puṇarcci urip poruḷai uṇarttum kuṟiñcip pāṭalkaḷ eṇpatāka amaintaṉa; pirivai uṇarttum pālaittiṇaip pāṭalkaḷ irunūṟāka uḷḷatu vāḻvil iṉpam aṭaiya tuṉpattil mikutiyum uḻala vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu eṉpatai uṇarttavō eṉṟu eṇṇat tōṉṟukiṟatu.
akattuṟaip pāṭalkaḷ nāṭakap pāṅkil amaintuḷḷaṉa. colla vanta karuttai nilai nāṭṭa uvamai mūlamāka viḷakkuvatu mikavum eḷitu. peṇkaḷ nayamākap pēcik karuttai valiyuṟuttic ceyal paṭuttuvatil vallavarkaḷ. akattuṟaiyil tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṉṟu pēcum pēccukaḷ tamiḻp paṇpāṭṭiṉ taṉittaṉmaiyaik kāṭṭuvaṉa. tōḻiyiṉ pēccil uḷḷuṟaip poruḷum iṟaiccip poruḷum veḷippaṭum pāṅku niṉaintu niṉaintu pōṟṟattakkaṉa.
uḷḷuṟai eṉpatu teyvam nīṅkalākak kūṟappaṭum. avvan nilattuk karup poruḷkaḷai nilaikkaḷaṉākak koṇṭu uṇarappaṭum kuṟippup poruḷākum. karup poruḷ nikaḻcacikaḷ uvamam pōla amaintu avaṟṟiṉ ottu muṭivatu pōlap peṟappaṭum oru karuttup poruḷākum.
iṟaicci tāṉē poruṭ puṟattatuvē eṉpār tolkāppiyar. iṟaicciyiṟ piṟakkum poruḷumāruḷavē iṟaicci tāṉē urip puṟattatuvē eṉṟum pāṭam. akattiṇaik karupporuḷkaḷiṉ mūlam peṟappaṭum kuṟippup poruḷ iṟaicci eṉṟu koḷḷalām. ak kuṟippup poruḷiliruntu vēṟu oru karuttup peṟappaṭumāyiṉ ak karuttē iṟaicciyil piṟakkum poruḷ eṉṟu koḷḷalām. akanāṉūṟṟil mutal karupporuḷkaḷukkē ciṟappiṭam koṭuttup pēcappaṭukiṟatu. mika nuṭpamāṉa uḷḷuṟai uvamamum iṟaiccip poruḷum āṅkāṅku kaṇṭu uṇarntu makiḻumāṟu amaintuḷḷaṉa. āciriyar caṅka ilakkiyac ceyyuḷkaḷil peritum payiṟciyuṭaiyavar eṉpatai avaratu urainaṭaiyāl kāṇamuṭikiṟatu. ceyyuḷ ilakkaṇam kaṭanta urainaṭaip pāṭṭu eṉṟu collumaḷavukkut toṭarkaḷ amain tuḷḷaṉa. nīṇṭa eccac coṟkaḷāl karuttait teḷivuṟuttum pāṅku ivvāciriyarkkē kaivanta kalaiyāka amaintu nammai ellām viyakka vaikkiṟatu.
caṅkak kālattil vaḻakkilirunta coṟkaḷai niṉaivuṟuttum pāṅkil ariya coṟkaḷait tamatu urainaṭaiyil kaiyāṇṭu tamiḻaip paḻam perumai kuṉṟāmal kāttiṭa ivaratu urainaṭai ciṟanta eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum eṉpatai inta akanāṉūṟṟu urainaṭaiyaip payilvār uṇarvar eṉpatu uṟuti.
ariya nayam mikka centamiḻt toṭaraiyum āciriyar tamatu uraiyil toṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār. meyyiṉ niḻal pōlat tiraṇṭa āyattōṭu viḷaiyāṭi makiḻvēṉ eṉṟu 49 ām pāṭalil kuṟippiṭu kiṟār. mey - uṇmai avaravar niḻal avaravarai viṭṭup piriyātu atu pōla talaiviyai viṭṭup piriyāta tōḻiyarōṭu talaivi viḷaiyāṭi yatai eṇṇi makiḻalām.
uppuviṟkum peṇ ‘nellukku veḷḷuppu’ eṉṟu kūvik kai vīci naṭakkiṟāḷ. paṇṭa māṟṟu muṟaiyai itu namakku uṇarttukiṟatu.
nīṉiṟa vaṇṇaṉ kuṉiyā niṉṟa āyar peṇkaḷiṉ tukilai eṭuttuk koṇṭu kuruntamarat tēṟiṉāṉākap pāṉiṟa vaṇṇaṉ iṭaiyar kulap peṇkaḷiṉ māṉattaik kākkak kurunta marak kiḷaiyait tāḻttit taḻaikaḷāl maṟaittuk kāttāṉ eṉṟu kūṟum varalāṟṟaip pālait tiṇaic ceyyuḷ 59 il kūṟiyiruppatu eṇṇi makiḻattakkatu. peṇ yāṉai uṇpataṟku āṇ yāṉai yāmarattiṉ kiḷaiyait vaḷaittut tāḻttit taruvataik kaṇṭum, matanīr oḻukum kaṉṉattil moykkum vaṇṭukaḷai ōṭṭum taḻaikaḷiṉ ceyalaiyum oruṅku iṇaittup pārttut talaivaṉiṉ taṇṇaḷiyai eṇṇi āṟi irukkalām eṉṉum tōḻiyiṉ kūṟṟai mika āḻamāka āciriyar viḷakkiya pāṅku pōṟṟutaṟku uriyatām.
aṉpaṉ
pulavar ta. āṟumukaṉ
nūlaṟimukavurai
tiru. na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai 1930-40kaḷil tamiḻ, tamiḻilakkiyam, tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻar varalāṟu, tirāviṭa varalāṟu, tamiḻnāṭṭuk kuṭikaḷ pōṉṟa pala viṭayaṅkaḷ paṟṟi akkālattuc cātāraṇa tamiḻ vācakar nilaiyil peritum vācikkappaṭṭa nūlkaḷai eḻutiṉār. paṇṭaiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷāṉa akanāṉūṟu, kalittokai, paripāṭal pōṉṟa vaṟṟiṉai urainaṭaiyil eḻuti makkaḷiṭaiyē caṅka ilakkiyam paṟṟiya uṇarviṉai ēṟpaṭuttiṉār. tirukkuṟaḷukkāṉa oru collaṭaivait tirukkuṟaḷ akarāti eṉṉum peyaril veḷiyiṭṭavar. ivai yāvaṟṟukkum mēlāka ulaka varalāṟṟu nikaḻccikaḷaic curukkamākak kālavaricaippaṭuttit tantār. centamiḻ akarāti eṉṟavōr akara mutaliyaiyum tokuttār.
iṉṟu piṉṉōkkip pārkkum poḻutu 1940-50kaḷil iḷaiñar kaḷāka irunta pala tamiḻ ārvalarkaḷ tamiḻ paṟṟiya taṅkaḷ ārvattai vaḷarttuk koḷvataṟkāṉa vācippu viṭayaṅkaḷai aḷittavar. ivaruṭaiya perumpālāṉa paṭaippukkaḷ akkālattiṟ piracurikkap paṭṭa oṟṟumai eṉṉum itaḻiṉ aluvalakattālēyē veḷiyiṭap peṟṟaṉa. ivar veḷiyiṭṭaṉavaṟṟuḷ pala oṟṟumai itaḻil veḷivan tiruttal vēṇṭum. āṉāl, ataṉai ippoḻutu niccayamāka eṉṉāṟ colla muṭiyavillai. oṟṟumai aluvalakam ivaṟṟaip piracurittatu eṉpatai aṟivēṉ. ēṉeṉil ilaṅkaiyil tamiḻāciriyarāka irunta eṉatu takappaṉāriṭattu innūlkaḷiṟ perumpālāṉavai iruntaṉa.
tiru na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷaiyiṉ peyar tamiḻakattilē toṭarntu pōṟṟappaṭumaḷavukku īḻattil niṉaikkappaṭuvatillai eṉṟa uṇmaiyai mikunta maṉavaruttattuṭaṉ pativu ceyyavēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. aṅku avar attuṇaip pōṟṟappaṭātiruppataṟkuk kāraṇam ēṟattāḻa avarkaḷatu ellā nūlkaḷumē tamiḻakattilēyē veḷiyiṭap peṟṟaṉa.
in nūlkaḷiṉ piracurap piṉpulam paṟṟi etuvum teḷivākat teriyavillai. avaṟṟiṉ poruḷiyal amcaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya taravukaḷum ippoḻutu teriyātuḷḷaṉa.
īḻattut tamiḻilakkiya varalāṟṟiṟkūṭa ivaratu peyar mukkiyap paṭuttap peṟātu pōyuḷḷatu. mikunta ciramattiṉ piṉṉar avaraip paṟṟik kiṭaittuḷḷa takavalkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu:
cuvāmi ñāṉap pirakācar, na.ci. kantaiyā piḷḷai pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ tamiḻp paṅkaḷippukkaḷ tamiḻakattil pōṟṟappaṭukiṉṟamai īḻattavarkkup perumai tarukiṉṟatu. ivar eḻutiya nūlkaḷiṉ peyarai nōkkumpoḻutu tamiḻ vācakarkaḷukku ulaka nilaippaṭṭa, tamiḻnilaip paṭṭa taravukaḷait tokuttut taruvatē ivaratu peruñcirattaiyāka iruntatu eṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu. ippatippakattiṉ paṇiyiṉai ūkkuvikka vēṇṭiyatu tamiḻcārnta niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷiṉatum tamiḻp periyōrkaḷiṉatum kaṭamaiyākum. uṇmaiyil itaṉai oru aṟivuppacip piṇit tīrvākavē nāṉ kāṇkiṟēṉ. 
2/7, ṟāmskēṭ, aṉpuṭaṉ
58, 37āvatu oḻuṅkai, kārttikēcu civattampi
veḷḷavattai, takaicār ōyvunilai pērāciriyar
koḻumpu - 6 yāḻppāṇap palkalaik kaḻakam. 
karuvinūl tanta na.ci. kantaiyā
tamiḻ maṟumalarccikku vittiṭṭa mūlavarkaḷil yāḻppāṇam tanta pēraṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyāvum oruvar. uṇmaiyāṉa aṟiñarkaḷaik kālaṅkaṭantu aṭaiyāḷam kāṇpatum avartam paṭaippukkaḷait tēṭip piṭittup purappatum tamiḻiṉattiṉ paḻakkaṅkaḷil oṉṟu.
tamiḻiṉ, tamiḻariṉ tolpaḻaṅkāla varalāṟu toṭarpākat tamiḻil nūlkaḷ mikak kuṟaivu. ōr āyiram nūlkaḷāvatu tamiḻiṉ tamiḻariṉ tolpaḻaṅkāla varalāṟupaṟṟi veḷivaravēṇṭum. virivāka eḻutappaṭa vēṇṭiya tamiḻ, tamiḻar varalāṟṟu varaivu muyaṟcikku vaḻikāṭṭum karuvi nūlkaḷai varaintiruppavartāṉ na.ci. kantaiyā perumakaṉār.
ulakam muḻuvatum uṟṟuk kavaṉikka vēṇṭiya varalāṟṟiṟku uriyavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷ. avarkaḷē ulaka moḻikaḷai īṉṟa mūlamoḻikkuc contakkārarkaḷ. avarkaḷē ulaka nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ piṟappiṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa ulaka mutal nākarikattaip paṭaittavarkaḷ. inta uṇmaikaḷait tamiḻarkaḷum aṟiyavillai ulakamum aṟiyavillai.
tamiḻar carittiram, varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa paḻan tamiḻar, tamiḻar yār, ulaka nākarikattil tamiḻarpaṅku, cintuveḷit tamiḻar, tamiḻ intiyā, tamiḻakam, maṟainta nākarikaṅkaḷ ākiyaṉa na.ci. kantaiyāviṉ kuṟippiṭattakunta nūlkaḷākum.
ulaka mutal nākarikam eṉa iṉṟu ulakam nampikkoṇṭirukkak kūṭiya nīla āṟṟaṅkarai nākarikam (Nile Civilisation) tamiḻ nākarikattiṉ atāvatu cintuveḷi nākarikattiṉ vaḻippaṭṭa nākarikamē eṉpatai ērāḷamāṉa cāṉṟukaḷāl viḷakkupavai mēlaiya nūlkaḷ.
moḻinilaiyil tamiḻiṉ ulaka mutaṉmaiyaip pāvāṇar nilaināṭṭiṉār eṉṟāl iṉa nilaiyil tamiḻiṉa ulaka mutaṉmaiyai na.ci. kantaiyā nilaināṭṭiṉār eṉṟu uraikkalām.
nīla āṟṟaṅkarai nākarika muṭiviṉpiṉ naṇṇilak kaṭaṟ pakutiyil uruvāṉa piṟitoru vaḻinākarikamē kirēkka nākarikam. kirēkka nākarikat tiṉ uṭaivil teṟippil piṟaku malarntavaiyē iṉṟaiya mēlai nākarikam. ulakiṉ ellā nākarikaṅkaḷaiyum tāṅkiniṟkum tāy nākarikamē tamiḻ nākarikam.
tamiḻ nākarikat toṉmaiyaic caṅka nūlkaḷukku appāl cintuveḷi akaḻvāyvum ulaka varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ ulaka mutal nākarikam paṟṟit terivittirukkum karuttukkaḷum namakku mēlum viḷakkum pakutikaḷākat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. iṅkellām ceṉṟu nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipula muyaṟciyāl tamiḻiṉa varalāṟu eḻutiyavartāṉ na.ci. kantaiyā perumakaṉār.
na.ci. kantaiyā perumakaṉār nūlkaḷait tamiḻariṉ tolpaḻaṅkāla varalāṟṟaik kaṟkum muyaṟciyil nāṉ īṭupaṭattoṭaṅkiyapōtu tēṭik kaṟṟēṉ. paccaiyappaṉ kallūriyiṉ mēṉāḷ tamiḻp pērāciriyar ceṉṟa āṇṭu iyaṟkaiyeytiya varalāṟṟup pēraṟiñar kō. nilavaḻakaṉār na.ci. kantaiyā avarkaḷaic ceṉṉaiyil avar vāḻnta nāḷil cantittatu paṟṟiyum avariṉ paṉṉūṟpulamai paṟṟiyum emmiṭam makiḻntu kūṟuvār. na.ci. kantaiyā avarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷ palavaṟṟaiyum avar vaittiruntār. arukiya paḻaiya nūlkaḷaip pēṇuvāriṭattilum nūlakaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟilum maṭṭumē oṭuṅkikkiṭanta na.ci. kantaiyā nūlkaḷai mīṇṭum accil veḷiyiṭuvār ilarē eṉṟu kavalaiyuṟṟēṉ. amiḻtam patippakam ivariṉ nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭukiṉṟatu. uṇmait tamiḻ neñcaṅkaḷ in nūlkaḷai uccimōntu vāriyaṇaittup pukaḻntu kaṟṟup payaṉaṭaivārkaḷ eṉpatil eṉakku nampikkaiyuṇṭu.
pērā. ku. aracēntiraṉ
patippurai
vaḷam cērkkum paṇi
“kumarināṭṭiṉ tamiḻiṉap perumaiyai nimirac ceytāṉ,” “cintaiyum ceyalum centamiḻukku cērtta nūl oṉṟā iraṇṭā” eṉṟu pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ avarkaḷāl pōṟṟappaṭṭa tamiḻīḻa aṟiñar na.ci. kantaiyāviṉ nūlkaḷai mīṇṭum maṟupatippuc ceytu tamiḻ ulakiṟku vaḷam cērkkum paṇiyaic ceyya muṉ vantuḷḷōm.
ipperumakaṉār eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṟupatukku mēṟpaṭṭavai yākum. innūlkaḷ ciṟitum peritumāka uḷḷavai. ivaṟṟaip poruḷ vāriyākap pirittu irupatukku mēṟpaṭṭa nūl tiraṭṭukaḷākat tamiḻ ulakiṟkuk kaḷamākavum, taḷamākavum vaḷam cērkkum vairamaṇi mālai yākavum koṭuttuḷḷōm. moḻikkum iṉattiṟkum araṇāka amaiyum ivvaṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷ etirkālat tamiḻ ulakiṟkup perum payaṉait taravallaṉa.
na.ci. kantaiyā
ivar 1893il tamiḻīḻa maṇṇil navāliyūr eṉṉum ūril piṟantavar. piṟanta ūrilēyē paḷḷippaṭippai muṭittu avvūrilēyē āciriyap paṇiyāṟṟiyavar. piṉṉar malēciya maṇṇil cilakālam toṭarvaṇṭit tuṟaiyil paṇiyāṟṟiyuḷḷār. ivar tamiḻ īḻa maṇṇil piṟantiruntālum tamiḻakattil iruntutāṉ avar tamiḻ moḻikkum tamiḻ iṉattiṟkum perumai cērkkum nūlkaḷ pala eḻutiṉār eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
tamiḻukkut toṇṭāṟṟiya aṟiñarkaḷil na.ci. kantaiyā kuṟippiṭattakkavar. taṉṉai muṉṉilaip paṭuttātu moḻiyaiyum iṉattaiyum muṉṉilaippaṭuttiya perumaikkuriyavar. ulaka moḻi kaḷuḷ tamiḻmoḻi toṉmaimikkatu. ulakamoḻikaḷukku vērākavum cāṟākavum amaintatu. tamiḻar camayamum, kalaiyum paṇpāṭum, varalāṟum, tamiḻaṉ kaṇṭa aṟiviyalum ulakukku muṉṉōṭiyākat tikaḻvaṉa. ivaṟṟaiyellām tam nuṇṇaṟivāl kaṇṭaṟintu pala nūlkaḷai yāttavar.
tamiḻiyam paṟṟiya āyvai ārāynta aṟiñarkaḷ palaruḷar. ivarkaḷ aṉaivarum kalai, ilakkiyam, camayam, moḻi, varalāṟu, nākarikam, paṇpāṭu pōṉṟa pala tuṟaikaḷil paṅkāṟṟiyuḷḷaṉar. āṉāl, na.ci.ka. it tuṟaikaḷil maṭṭumaṉṟip potu aṟivut tuṟaiyilum pukuntu puttākkac ceytikaḷait tamiḻ ulakiṟkuk koṭuttavar. ivaruṭaiya moḻipeyarppu paṭippārai īrkkak kūṭiyavai. paṭittaliṉ nōkkam paṟṟiyum palvēṟu poruḷ paṟṟiyum kūṟupavai. tamiḻ akarātit tuṟaiyil ivar eḻutiya kālakkuṟippu akarāti tamiḻ ulakiṟkup putuvaravāy amaintatu.
tamiḻ moḻikku ariya nūlkaḷait tantōr mikac cilarē. niṟaitamiḻ aṟiñar maṟaimalai aṭikaḷum, tamiḻ malaiyām tēvanēyap pāvāṇarum tamiḻ moḻikku ākkamum ūkkamum cērkkattakka nūlkaḷait tamiḻarkaḷukku vaippāka eḻutic ceṉṟavarkaḷ. avar tam varicaiyil ivar tam nūlkaḷiṉ varicaiyum aṭaṅkum. tamiḻ āyvu varalāṟṟil ivariṉ paṅkaḷippu kuṟikkattakkatu. kaḻakak kālac ceyti kaḷaip paṭittup poruḷ purintu koḷvatu palarukkuk kaṭiṉamāka iruntatu. ivaṟṟai eḷitil paṭittup poruḷ puriyum urainaṭaip pōkkai mutaṉmutalil kaiyāṇṭavar. ivaratu moḻinaṭai, karuttukku mutaṉmai tarupavai. urainaṭai, eḷimaiyum teḷivum uṭaiyatu. ciṟuciṟu vākkiya amaippil teḷinta nīrōṭai pōṉṟatu. īrāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ teṉṉakam peṟṟirunta nākarikap paṇpāṭṭuk kūṟukaḷaik kaṇṇukkuk kāṭciyākavum paṭippāriṉ karuttukku viruntākavum aḷittavar.
nūl tiraṭṭukaḷ nuvalum ceytikaḷ
1800 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻar kālam toṭṭu ivar vāḻnta kālam varai tamiḻariṉ varalāṟṟuc cuvaṭukaḷai nuṭpamāka ārāyn tuḷḷār. avar maṟaiviṟkup piṟaku iṉṟu varai avaruṭaiya āyviṟku mēlum ākkam tarum ceytikaḷ aṟivulakil tamiḻukkum, tamiḻarkaḷukkum valuvāy amaintuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻum civaneṟiyum ōṅkiyiruntamai. - tāyāṭcik kālam mutaṉmai peṟṟiruntatu. moḻiyiṉ tōṟṟam, camaṟkirutam eppaṭit tōṉṟiyatu - āriyar yār - intiyāviṟku eppaṭi vantaṉar - tamiḻarōṭu evvāṟu kalappuṟṟaṉar - ātimakkaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum, piṟappum - intiya nāṭṭiṉ ātimakkaḷ, tirāviṭa makkaḷ - ātimakkaḷ piṟanāṭukaḷil kuṭipeyarntatu - moḻikkum, camayattukkum uḷḷa uṟavu - camaṟkirutam camayamoḻi āṉataṟkāṉa āyvukaḷ - vaḻipāṭṭiṉ toṭakkam - vaḻipāṭu eṅkellām iruntatu - paṇṭaiya makkaḷ eḻutu vataṟkup payaṉpaṭuttiya poruḷkaḷ - mēlai nāṭukaḷilum, ceṉṉai yilum irunta nūl nilaiyaṅkaḷ - āriyamoḻi intiya maṇṇil vērūṉṟiya varalāṟu - tamiḻukku nērnta kēṭukaḷ - tiruvaḷḷuvar kuṟitta kataikaḷ - vaḷḷuvac camayam - paṇṭaiya nākarika nāṭukaḷ - annāṭukaḷil peṇkaḷaip paṟṟiya nilai - akattiyar paṟṟiya paḻaṅkataikaḷ - tirāviṭa moḻikaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ - ulakanāṭukaḷil tamiḻar nākarikac cuvaṭukaḷ - tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻi tamiḻ - tirāviṭariṉ piṟappiṭam - aivakai nilaṅkaḷ, pākupāṭukaḷ - paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ - cintu veḷi nākarika mēṉmai - puttariṉ piṟappu - avaraip paṟṟiya kataikaḷ - irāmakātai paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ - teṉṉavariṉ kulaṅkaḷ, kuṭikaḷ, vaṭavariṉ kulaṅkaḷ, .kuṭikaḷ - tamiḻiṉ paḻamai, tamiḻaka ellai amaippu - paṇṭaikkāla kalvi muṟai, urainaṭai - vētaṅkaḷ, vētaṅkaḷukkum ākamaṅkaḷukkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭukaḷ - nīrvaḻi, nilavaḻi vaṇikam ciṟantōṅkiya nilai - malaivaḻi, kaṭalvaḻi, nilavaḻi poruḷkaḷ nirampiya kālam - cuṟṟam taḻaikka vāḻnta nilai - tamiḻa vaṇikar, vēṟṟu nāṭṭu vaṇikaruṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭu mikkōṅkiyirunta kālam - vāṉanūl kalaiyum, ciṟpak kalaiyum, kaṭṭaṭak kalaiyum , icaikkalaiyum mikkōṅkiyirunta kālam - āriyar varukaikku muṟpaṭṭa piṟamoḻi kalappaṟṟa tūya tamiḻ peruki yirunta kālam - ulaka nākarikaṅkaḷukkut tamiḻar nākarikam nāṟṟaṅkāl - ulakap paṇpāṭukaḷukkut tamiḻar paṇpāṭu toṭṭilāka amaintamai - akarātikaḷ - aṟivut tēṭalukkuriya ceytikaḷ - ivartam nūlkaḷiṉ uyirkkūṟukaḷāka aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa.
vāḻum moḻi tamiḻ
tamiḻ iḷaiñarkaḷ tam muṉṉōriṉ perumaiyai uṇara, etirkāla vāḻviṟku ēṇippaṭikaḷāy amaivaṉa innūlkaḷ. ekiptiya moḻi, cumēriya moḻi, ilattīṉum, pāliyum kirēkkamum arapik moḻiyum vāḻntu ciṟanta kālattil tamiḻ moḻiyum vāḻntu ciṟantatu. paḻamperum moḻikaḷ pala māṇṭum cila kāppaka moḻikaḷākavum aṟiñarkaḷiṉ pārvai moḻiyākavum iruntu varum ik kālattil iṉṟaḷavum iḷamai kuṉṟā vaḷam niṟainta moḻiyāka tamiḻ moḻi vāḻkiṟatu eṉṟu nām perumaikoḷḷalām.
āṉāl, ittamiḻ moḻiyiṉ nilai iṉṟu āṭcimoḻiyāka aluval moḻiyāka, icaimoḻiyāka, kalvi moḻiyāka, aṟamaṉṟa moḻiyāka , vaḻipāṭṭu moḻiyāka, kuṭumpa moḻiyāka illāta iraṅkat takka nilaiyāka uḷḷatu. tāymoḻiyiṉ ciṟappaip puṟantaḷḷi vēṟṟumoḻiyait tūkkip piṭikkum avala nilai mikuntuḷḷatu. mukattai iḻanta māntaṉ uyirvāḻa muṭiyātu. eppaṭi uyiraṟṟavaṉō, avvāṟē moḻiyai iḻanta iṉam irunta iṭam teriyāmal aḻiyum eṉpatu ulaka varalāṟu kāṭṭum uṇmai. oru iṉattai aḻikka vēṇṭumeṉil moḻiyai aḻittāltāṉ iṉattai aḻikka muṭiyum. ulaka makkaḷellām tam kaiyeḻuttait tam tam tāymoḻiyil pōṭuvataip perumaiyākak koḷvar. āṉāl tamiḻ maṇṇiṉ nilaiyō? eṅkaṇum kāṇāta avalam niṟaintatu. moḻiyaiyum iṉattaiyum uyarvākak karutiya iṉaṅkaḷ ulaka araṅkil uyarntu niṟkum uṉṉatattaiyum tāḻvākak karutiya iṉaṅkaḷ ulaka araṅkil tāḻvuṟṟu iruppataiyum tamiḻarkaḷ iṉiyēṉum aṟivārkaḷā?
tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kaṭaṉ
iḷam talaimuṟaikkum māṇavarkaḷukkum payaṉpaṭattakka ivvariya nūlkaḷait tokuttu 23 tiraṭṭukaḷākak koṭuttuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ kāppukkum, tamiḻariṉ eḻuccikkum vittiṭum innūlkaḷ. tamiḻar yār, etirikaḷ yār eṉum ariya uṇmaikaḷaik kaṇṭu kāṭṭum nūlkaḷ. ivariṉ pēruḻaippāl eḻutappaṭṭa in nūlkaḷ paḻaiya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷai mīṭṭeṭukkum nūlkaḷ. tamiḻar kaḷukkuḷḷa palavīṉattai uṇarvataṟkum palattai uyirppippataṟkum uriya nūlkaḷākum. in nūlkaḷait tēṭi eṭuttu iḷantalaimuṟaikku vaippākak koṭuttuḷḷōm. ivaṟṟaik kāppatum pōṟṟuvatum tamiḻarkaḷ kaṭaṉ. 
māṇṭupōṉa icurēl moḻiyaiyum paṇpāṭṭaiyum mīṭ ṭeṭutta icurēliyarkaḷiṉ varalāṟu namakkup pāṭamāka amaintuḷḷatu. ulaka makkaḷukku nākarikam iṉṉateṉak kāṭṭiyavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷ. ulakil mutaṉmutalil uḻavut toḻilaiyum kaṭal vāḻvaiyum vaḷartta varkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷ. mutaṉmutalil vīṭamaippum, teruvamaippum nakaramaippum nāṭamaippum kaṇṭavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷ. ulakil mutaṉmutalil moḻiyum kalaiyum āṭcip pirivukaḷum, caṭṭaṅkaḷum piṟa kūṟukaḷum vakuttavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷ.
eḻuccikku vittiṭa...
uṭampu nōkāmal kai nakattiṉ kaṇkaḷil aḻukkupaṭāmal tamiḻakak kōyilkaḷaic cāḷaramākak koṇṭu vāḻum kūṭṭattāl tamiḻukkum tamiḻarkaḷukkum viḷainta cīraḻivai iṟṟait talaimuṟai aṟital vēṇṭum. maṟumalarccikkum urimaip pōrukkum uṉṉata eḻuccikkum antanta nāṭukaḷil iḷaiñarkaḷ muṉṉeṭuttuc ceṉṟa varalāṟu nam kaṇmuṉṉē kāṭciyākat terikiṟatu. ayarlāntu, cermaṉi, turukki, cappāṉiṉ aṉṟaiya nilaiyum, iṉṟaiya nilaiyum - tamiḻ iḷaiñarkaḷ paṭittāltāṉ namnāṭṭiṉ eḻuccikku vittiṭa muṭiyum eṉpatai innūlkaḷiṉ vāyilāka uṇara muṭikiṟatu.
in nūl tiraṭṭukaḷ veḷivaruvataṟku eṉakkup peritum utaviyāka iruntavar ceṉṉai vāḻ pulavarum, vaṭaceṉṉai tamiḻ vaḷarccip pēravaiyiṉ ceyalāḷar, niṟuvaṉarumāṉa pulavar kō. tēvarācaṉ, mu.ka.,ka.i., āvār. ivariṉ pērutaviyāl muḻumaiyāka nūlkaḷait tēṭi eṭuttut tamiḻ ulakiṟkuk koṭaiyākak koṭuttuḷḷōm. avarukku em naṉṟi. innūlkaḷaip poruḷ vāriyāka pirittut tiraṭṭukaḷāka ākkiyuḷḷōm. ovvoru tiraṭṭiṟkum takka tamiḻc cāṉṟōriṉ aṟimuka uraiyōṭu veḷiyiṭukiṟōm. ivarkaḷukku eṉ naṉṟi eṉṟum. in nūlākkap paṇikku utaviya kō. araṅkarācaṉ, mēlaṭṭai ākkattiṟku utaviya pirēm, kaṇiṉi iyakkunarkaḷ caravaṇaṉ, kuppucāmi, kalaiyaracaṉ, kaṭṭunar taṉacēkaraṉ, innūlkaḷ piḻaiyiṉṟi veḷivara meypput tirutti utaviya pulavar cīṉivācaṉ, pulavar āṟumukam, celvarācaṉ ākiyōrukkum maṟṟum acciṭṭu utaviya ‘prāmṭ’ accakattār maṟṟum ‘veṅka ṭēcuvarā’ accakattārukkum em patippakam cārpil naṉṟiyait terivittuk koḷkiṟēṉ.
